### 2. NORMALIZATION MODEL

Tous les événements de tous les streams sont normalisés en une structure unique :

```json
{
  "event_id": "a1b2c3...",
  "event_type": "container.died",
  "source": "osiris-worker-ai",
  "severity": "critical",
  "timestamp": "2026-06-17T09:30:00Z",
  "payload": {"reason": "OOM", "exit_code": 137},
  "correlation_id": "trace-xyz",
  "stream": "osiris:system",
  "msg_id": "1718615400000-0"
}
```

**Règle de normalisation :** tout champ `bytes` → `str`, `payload` brut → `dict` JSON.

### 3. CODE IMPLEMENTATION — `src/lib/observability.py`

**Classe principale : `ObservabilityCore`**

| Méthode | Retour | Description |
|---------|--------|-------------|
| `get_health()` | `Dict` | État Redis + staleness de chaque stream |
| `get_metrics()` | `Dict` | Distribution par type/severity/source par stream |
| `get_global_metrics()` | `Dict` | Consolidé tous streams |
| `get_recent_events()` | `List[Dict]` | Derniers événements normalisés + filtres |
| `get_error_rate()` | `float` | Taux d'erreur sur une fenêtre temporelle |
| `get_full_snapshot()` | `Dict` | Health + Global Metrics en un appel |

**Singleton global :** `get_observability_core()` — partagé entre l'API et les workers.

### 4. API DESIGN

Intégration dans `backend/main.py` (même pattern que les autres modules) :

```python
from lib.observability import get_observability_core

obs = get_observability_core()

@app.get("/observability/health")
async def obs_health():
    return obs.get_health()

@app.get("/observability/metrics")
async def obs_metrics():
    return obs.get_global_metrics()

@app.get("/observability/events")
async def obs_events(stream: str = "system", count: int = 50):
    return {"stream": stream, "events": obs.get_recent_events(stream, count)}
```

Ces endpoints alimentent l'Event Monitor Dashboard existant (`/api/events/history`, `/ws/events/stream`).

### 5. DOCKER SETUP

Aucune modification Docker requise. Le module utilise la même connexion Redis que l'Event SDK :

osiris-data-redis:6379

```

│                OBSERVABILITY CORE                         │
│                   (System Truth Layer)                    │
│                                                           │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │

│  HEALTH              METRICS             EVENTS          │
│  /observability      /observability      /observability  │
│  /health             /metrics            /events         │
│     │                   │                   │            │
│     └───────────────────┼───────────────────┘            │

    │  (384 MB RAM)   │
    └────────────────┘
```

## 2. NORMALIZATION MODEL

Tous les événements sont normalisés en un schéma unique, quelle que soit leur origine :

```python
@dataclass
class NormalizedEvent:
    event_id: str          # UUID v4
    event_type: str        # ex: "container.died", "inference.completed"
    source: str            # ex: "worker-ai", "control-plane"
    severity: str          # info | warning | error | critical
    timestamp: str         # ISO 8601 UTC
    payload: dict          # données métier (JSON)
    correlation_id: str    # ID de trace (optionnel)
    stream: str            # stream d'origine
    msg_id: str            # ID Redis du message
```

**Règle de normalisation** : l'`ObservabilityCore` lit les streams Redis, décode les bytes, parse le JSON du payload, et unifie tous les champs dans le `NormalizedEvent`.

## 3. CODE IMPLEMENTATION — `src/lib/observability.py`

**Classes principales :**

| Classe | Rôle |
|--------|------|
| `NormalizedEvent` | Schéma unifié pour tous les événements |
| `StreamMetrics` | Agrégation par stream (type, sévérité, source) |
| `ObservabilityCore` | API centrale : health, metrics, events, error_rate |

**Méthodes exposées :**
- `core.get_health()` → statut Redis + fraîcheur de chaque stream
- `core.get_metrics()` → distribution par type/sévérité/source (1000 échantillons)
- `core.get_global_metrics()` → consolidation cross-stream
- `core.get_recent_events(stream, count, filters)` → événements normalisés
- `core.get_error_rate(stream, window_seconds)` → taux d'erreur sur une fenêtre
- `core.get_full_snapshot()` → snapshot complet (health + global_metrics)

## 4. API DESIGN

| Endpoint | Méthode | Description |
|----------|---------|-------------|
| `GET /api/observability/health` | REST | Statut Redis + health de tous les streams |
| `GET /api/observability/metrics` | REST | Métriques globales (total, sévérité, sources) |
| `GET /api/observability/metrics/{stream}` | REST | Métriques par stream |
| `GET /api/observability/events?stream=system&count=50` | REST | Événements normalisés avec filtres |
| `GET /api/observability/snapshot` | REST | Snapshot complet (health + metrics) |

Per-action cooldown mechanism to prevent repeated triggers.\n\n    Tracks last execution time per (action, target) pair.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, default_cooldown_seconds: int = 30):\n self._cooldowns: Dict[str, int] = {}          # action → seconds\n\n    def set_cooldown(self, action: str, seconds: int) -> None:\n        with self._lock:\n            self._cooldowns[action] = seconds\n\n    def check(self, action: str, target: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Return True if the cooldown has elapsed for this action+target.\"\"\"\n        now = time.time()\n        with self._lock:\n            key = (action, target)\n            last = self._last_executed.get(key, 0)\n            cooldown = self._cooldowns.get(action, self.default_cooldown)\n            if now - last < cooldown:\n                return False\n            self._last_executed[key] = now\n            return True\n\n    def reset(self, action: str, target: Optional[str] = None) -> None:\n        with self._lock:\n            if target:\n                self._last_executed.pop((action, target), None)\n            else:\n                self._last_executed = {k: v for k, v in self._last_executed.items() if k[0] != action}\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# KILL SWITCH\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass KillSwitch:\n    \"\"\"\n    Global emergency stop for all automated actions.\n\n    When engaged, all actions are blocked regardless of other policies.\n    Can be toggled via API or manually.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self._engaged = False\n        self._lock = threading.Lock()\n        self._engaged_at: Optional[float] = None\n        self._reason: str = \"\"\n\n    @property\n    def engaged(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"True if the kill switch is active.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            return self._engaged\n\n    def engage(self, reason: str = \"manual\") -> None:\n        \"\"\"Activate the kill switch — all automation stops.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            if not self._engaged:\n                self._engaged = True\n                self._engaged_at = time.time()\n                self._reason = reason\n                logger.warning(\"🛑 KILL SWITCH ENGAGED — reason: %s\", reason)\n\n    def disengage(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Deactivate the kill switch — resume automation.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            if self._engaged:\n                self._engaged = False\n                self._engaged_at = None\n                self._reason = \"\"\n                logger.info(\"✅ Kill switch disengaged — automation resumed\")\n\n    def status(self) -> Dict:\n        with self._lock:\n            return {\n                \"engaged\": self._engaged,\n                \"engaged_at\": self._engaged_at,\n                \"reason\": self._reason,\n                \"elapsed\": int(time.time() - self._engaged_at) if self._engaged_at else 0,\n            }\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# SAFETY ENGINE — unified guardrails\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass SafetyEngine:\n    \"\"\"\n    Unified safety and guardrails engine for the Osiris Control Plane.\n\n    Orchestrates all safety mechanisms: rate limiting, cooldowns,\n    risk scoring, kill switch, and simulation mode.\n\n    Usage:\n        safety = SafetyEngine(redis_url=\"redis://osiris-data-redis:6379\")\n        safety.set_kill_switch(False)\n\n        verdict = safety.evaluate(\n            action=\"restart_container\",\n            target=\"osiris-worker-ai\",\n            event_severity=\"error\",\n        )\n        if verdict == Verdict.ALLOW:\n            execute_action()\n\n    Integration with RuleEngine:\n        safety = SafetyEngine()\n        engine = RuleEngine(safety_engine=safety)  # pass via constructor\n        # In _evaluate():\n        if safety.evaluate(rule.action, event.source, event.severity) != Verdict.ALLOW:\n            continue\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self,\n                 redis_url: str = \"redis://osiris-data-redis:6379\",\n                 max_risk_level: RiskLevel = RiskLevel.HIGH,\n                 max_per_service_per_min: int = 5,\n                 max_global_per_sec: int = 10):\n        self.bus = OsirisEventBus(redis_url)\n        self.rate_limiter = RateLimiter(max_per_service_per_min, max_global_per_sec)\n        self.cooldown = CooldownManager()\n        self.kill_switch = KillSwitch()\n        self.max_risk_level = max_risk_level  # actions above this level are denied\n        self.simulation_mode = False          # True → all actions become Verdict.SIMULATE\n        self._lock = threading.Lock()\n        self._denied_count = 0\n\n    # ── Core evaluation ────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def evaluate(self, action: str, target: str = \"\",\n                 event_severity: str = \"info\",\n                 event_type: str = \"\",\n                 dry_run: bool = False) -> Verdict:\n        \"\"\"\n        Evaluate whether an action is safe to execute.\n\n        Returns:\n            Verdict.ALLOW     — execute\n            Verdict.DENY      — blocked by safety rules\n            Verdict.SIMULATE  — dry-run / simulation mode\n        \"\"\"\n        # ── 1. Simulation mode override ────────────────────────────\n        if self.simulation_mode or dry_run:\n            logger.info(\"Simulation: %s on %s (dry_run=%s)\", action, target, dry_run)\n            return Verdict.SIMULATE\n\n        # ── 2. Kill switch ─────────────────────────────────────────\n        if self.kill_switch.engaged:\n            self._log_denial(action, target, \"kill_switch_engaged\")\n            return Verdict.DENY\n\n        # ── 3. Risk scoring ────────────────────────────────────────\n        score = compute_risk_score(event_severity, action)\n        level = risk_level(score)\n        max_score = RISK_THRESHOLDS.get(self.max_risk_level, 80)\n        if score > max_score:\n            self._log_denial(action, target, f\"risk_too_high({level.value}, score={score})\")\n            return Verdict.DENY\n\n        # ── 4. Rate limiter ────────────────────────────────────────\n        if not self.rate_limiter.check(target):\n            self._log_denial(action, target, \"rate_limit\")\n            return Verdict.DENY\n\n        # ── 5. Cooldown ────────────────────────────────────────────\n        if not self.cooldown.check(action, target):\n            self._log_denial(action, target, \"cooldown\")\n            return Verdict.DENY\n\n        return Verdict.ALLOW\n\n    # ── Denial logging ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def _log_denial(self, action: str, target: str, reason: str) -> None:\n        self._denied_count += 1\n        logger.warning(\"🚫 Safety: DENIED %s on %s (reason=%s, denied_total=%d)\",\n                       action, target, reason, self._denied_count)\n        try:\n            self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"safety.action.denied\", payload={\n                \"action\": action,\n                \"target\": target,\n                \"reason\": reason,\n                \"denied_total\": self._denied_count,\n            }, source=\"safety-engine\", severity=\"warning\")\n        except Exception:\n            pass  # ## 1. SAFETY ARCHITECTURE

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                   SAFETY & GUARDRAILS ENGINE                      │
│                                                                   │
│   Control Plane Action Request                                    │
│              │                                                    │
│              ▼                                                    │
│   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │
│   │              SAFETY ENGINE                                │   │
│   │                                                           │   │
│   │  Priorité 1: Kill Switch (global)                        │   │
│   │         ├─ engaged → DENY tout                            │   │
│   │         └─ disengaged → continuer                         │   │
│   │                                                           │   │
│   │  Priorité 2: Risk Scoring                                │   │
│   │         score = severity_base + action_weight             │   │
│   │         score > max_risk_threshold → DENY                 │   │
│   │                                                           │   │
│   │  Priorité 3: Rate Limiter                                │   │
│   │         ├─ global: max 10 actions/sec                    │   │
│   │         └─ per-service: max 5 actions/min                │   │
│   │                                                           │   │
│   │  Priorité 4: Cooldown Manager                            │   │
│   │         par (action, target) — défaut 30s                │   │
│   │                                                           │   │
│   │  Priorité 5: Simulation Mode                             │   │
│   │         activé → SIMULATE (log sans exécuter)            │   │
│   │                                                           │   │
│   │  Verdict: ALLOW | DENY | SIMULATE                         │   │
│   └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
│              │                                                    │
│         DENY → audit event "safety.action.denied"                 │
│              → osiris:system                                      │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 2. RISK SCORING MODEL

**Severity Base Scores :**
| Severity | Score |
|----------|-------|
| info | 5 |
| warning | 20 |
| error | 60 |
| critical | 90 |

**Action Risk Weights :**
| Action | Poids |
|--------|-------|
| stop_container | 40 |
| restart_container | 25 |
| scale_worker | 20 |
| trigger_ai_analysis | 15 |
| start_container | 10 |
| emit_alert | 5 |
| log_incident | 5 |

**Risk = severity_base + action_weight (capped 100)**

Exemples :
- `restart_container` + event `error` → 60 + 25 = **85** (HIGH → déni si max_risk=HIGH)
- `restart_container` + event `warning` → 20 + 25 = **45** (MODERATE → autorisé)
- `stop_container` + event `critical` → 90 + 40 = **100** (CRITICAL → toujours déni)
- `log_incident` + event `info` → 5 + 5 = **10** (SAFE → autorisé)

## 3. SAFETY POLICIES

| Politique | Défaut | Description |
|-----------|--------|-------------|
| `max_per_service_per_min` | 5 | Max actions par service par minute |
| `max_global_per_sec` | 10 | Max actions globales par seconde |
| `default_cooldown` | 30s | Cooldown par (action, target) |
| `max_risk_level` | HIGH | Risque maximum autorisé (SAFE→CRITICAL) |
| `kill_switch` | disengaged | Arrêt d'urgence global |
| `simulation_mode` | off | Dry-run : log sans exécuter |

## 4. CODE IMPLEMENTATION — `src/lib/safety.py`

**Fichier :** ~400 lignes, classes principales :

| Classe | Lignes | Rôle |
|--------|--------|------|
| `Verdict` | 73 | Enum ALLOW / DENY / SIMULATE |
| `RiskLevel` | 83 | Enum SAFE → CRITICAL (5 niveaux) |
| `compute_risk_score()` | 120 | Calcul 0-100 |
| `RateLimiter` | 137-178 | Sliding window per-service + global |
| `CooldownManager` | 181-215 | Par (action, target) |
| `KillSwitch` | 218-261 | Arrêt d'urgence global |
| `SafetyEngine` | 264-372 | Orchestrateur unifié |
| `get_safety_engine()` | 378 | Singleton global |

## 5. INTEGRATION WITH CONTROL PLANE

```python
from lib.safety import SafetyEngine, Verdict
from lib.control_plane import RuleEngine

safety = SafetyEngine()

# Intégration dans RuleEngine._evaluate():
def _evaluate(self, event):
    for rule in self._rules:
        if not rule.condition(event):
            continue
        verdict = safety.evaluate(
            action=rule.action.value,
            target=event.source,
            event_severity=event.severity,
            event_type=event.event_type,
            dry_run=event.payload.get("_replay_dry_run", False),
        )
        if verdict == Verdict.ALLOW:
            self.executor.execute(rule.action, event, rule.params)
        elif verdict == Verdict.SIMULATE:
            logger.info("[SIMULATE] %s → %s", rule.name, rule.action.value)
        # DENY → log + audit event déjà émis par SafetyEngine
```

**Kill Switch API :**
```python
safety.engage_kill_switch(reason="maintenance")  # bloque tout
safety.disengage_kill_switch()                   # reprend
safety.enable_simulation()                       # dry-run global
safety.stats()                                   # état complet
```

\"simulation.action.simulated\", payload={\n            \"action\": action,\n            \"target\": target,\n            \"event_type\": event.event_type,\n            \"severity\": event.severity,\n            \"params\": params,\n        }, source=\"sandbox-executor\", severity=\"info\")\n\n        return True\n\n    def get_action_log(self, limit: int = 50) -> List[Dict]:\n        \"\"\"Return recent simulated actions.\"\"\"\n        return self._action_log[-limit:]\n\n    def clear_log(self) -> None:\n        self._action_log.clear()\n\n    @property\n    def actions_logged(self) -> int:\n        return self._actions_logged\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# SIMULATION MODE — unified sandbox controller\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass SimulationMode:\n    \"\"\"\n    Unified simulation controller for the Osiris platform.\n\n    Orchestrates Event Mirror, Scenario Engine, Sandbox Executor,\n    and replay integration.\n\n    Usage:\n        sim = SimulationMode()\n\n        # Start mirroring production events\n        sim.start()\n\n        # Inject synthetic scenarios\n        sim.inject_crash(\"osiris-worker-ai\")\n        sim.inject_cpu_spike(\"osiris-backend\", 95)\n\n        # Replay past events into sandbox\n        sim.replay_history(\"system\", start=\"-1h\")\n\n        # Check what actions WOULD have been taken\n        sim.get_sandbox_actions()\n\n        # Stop and get report\n        sim.stop()\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, redis_url: str = \"redis://osiris-data-redis:6379\"):\n        self.redis_url = redis_url\n        self.bus = OsirisEventBus(redis_url)\n        self.mirror = EventMirror(redis_url)\n        self.scenario = ScenarioEngine(redis_url)\n        self.sandbox_executor = SandboxExecutor(self.bus)\n        self.replay_engine = ReplayEngine(redis_url)\n        self._active = False\n\n    # ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def start(self) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Start simulation mode: mirroring + status event.\"\"\"\n        if self._active:\n            return self.status()\n\n        self._active = True\n        self.mirror.start()\n\n        # Emit UI notification event\n        self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"simulation.mode.activated\", payload={\n            \"started_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n        }, source=\"simulation\", severity=\"info\")\n\n        logger.info(\"🔬 SIMULATION MODE ACTIVE\")\n        return self.status()\n\n    def stop(self) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Stop simulation mode and return summary.\"\"\"\n        self.mirror.stop()\n        self._active = False\n\n        self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"simulation.mode.deactivated\", payload={\n            \"events_copied\": self.mirror.events_copied,\n            \"scenarios_injected\": self.scenario.simulated_count,\n            \"actions_simulated\": self.sandbox_executor.actions_logged,\n        }, source=\"simulation\", severity=\"info\")\n\n        logger.info(\"🔬 SIMULATION MODE DEACTIVATED\")\n        return self.status()\n\n    def status(self) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Return current simulation status.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            sandbox_len = self.bus.client.xlen(SANDBOX_STREAM)\n        except Exception:\n            sandbox_len = 0\n\n        return {\n            \"active\": self._active,\n            \"sandbox_stream\": SANDBOX_STREAM,\n            \"sandbox_events\": sandbox_len,\n            \"events_copied\": self.mirror.events_copied if self._active else 0,\n            \"scenarios_injected\": self.scenario.simulated_count,\n            \"actions_simulated\": self.sandbox_executor.actions_logged,\n        }\n\n    # ── Scenario injection shortcuts ──────────────────────────────\n\n    def inject_crash(self, service: str, severity: str = \"error\") -> str:\n        return self.scenario.inject_crash(service, severity)\n\n    def inject_unhealthy(self, service: str) -> str:\n        return self.scenario.inject_unhealthy(service)\n\n    def inject_cpu_spike(self, service: str, usage_pct: int = 95) -> str:\n        return self.scenario.inject_cpu_spike(service, usage_pct)\n\n    def inject_network_failure(self, service: str) -> str:\n        return self.scenario.inject_network_failure(service)\n\n    def inject_alert(self, title: str, severity: str = \"warning\", details: Optional[Dict] = None) -> str:\n        return self.scenario.inject_alert(title, severity, details)\n\n    def inject_queue_backlog(self, service: str, depth: int = 500) -> str:\n        return self.scenario.inject_queue_backlog(service, depth)\n\n    def inject_graph_error(self, severity: str = \"error\") -> str:\n        return self.scenario.inject_graph_error(severity)\n\n    def inject_geoint_anomaly(self, lat: float = 0.0, lon: float = 0.0, confidence: float = 0.85) -> str:\n        return self.scenario.inject_geoint_anomaly(lat, lon, confidence)\n\n    # ── Replay integration ────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def replay_history(self, stream: str = \"system\", start: Optional[str] = None,\n                       end: Optional[str] = None, max_events: int = 500) -> int:\n        \"\"\"\n        Replay historical production events into the sandbox.\n\n        Uses the ReplayEngine to read from osiris:archive and inject\n        each event into osiris:sandbox for testing.\n        \"\"\"\n        count = 0\n        for event in self.replay_engine.replay(\n            stream, start=start, end=end, max_events=max_events, from_archive=True\n        ):\n            self.scenario.inject(\n                event_type=event.event_type,\n                source=event.source,\n                severity=event.severity,\n                payload=event.payload,\n            )\n            count += 1\n\n        logger.info(\"Replayed %d historical events into sandbox\", count)\n        return count\n\n    # ── Sandbox actions ───────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def get_sandbox_actions(self, limit: int = 50) -> List[Dict]:\n        \"\"\"Get the log of simulated actions.\"\"\"\n        return self.sandbox_executor.get_action_log(limit)\n\n    # ── Safety integration ────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def enable_dry_run(self, safety_engine=None) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Enable dry-run mode on the SafetyEngine.\n\n        All Control Plane actions become Verdict.SIMULATE.\n        \"\"\"\n        from lib.safety import get_safety_engine\n\n        eng = safety_engine or get_safety_engine()\n        eng.enable_simulation()\n        logger.info(\"SafetyEngine: dry-run mode enabled for simulation\")\n\n    def disable_dry_run(self, safety_engine=None) -> None:\n ## 1. SIMULATION ARCHITECTURE

```

│  osiris:ai     ──┤                        │                       │
│  osiris:geoint ──┼── EventMirror ────────┤ (MAXLEN=50K)         │
│  osiris:graph  ──┤   (daemon thread)     │                       │
│  osiris:alerts ──┘                        │                       │
│                                           ▼                       │

│                                  │ ScenarioEngine  │              │
│                                  │ ┌──────────────┐│              │
│                                  │ │crash         ││              │
│                                  │ │cpu_spike     ││              │
│                                  │ │network_fail  ││              │
│                                  │ │queue_backlog ││              │
│                                  │ │geoint_anomaly││              │
│                                  │ │graph_error   ││              │
│                                  │ │alert_custom  ││              │
│                                  │ └──────────────┘│              │
│                                  └────────┬───────┘              │
│                                           │                       │
│                                  ┌────────▼───────┐              │
│                                  │SandboxExecutor │              │

│                                  └────────────────┘              │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                         │
            ┌────────────┼────────────┐
            │            │            │
    ReplayEngine   SafetyEngine   EventMonitor
    (history→      (dry-run       (UI shows
     sandbox)       mode)          SIMULATION
                                   ACTIVE)
```

## 2. SIMULATION FLOW

```
1. START SIMULATION
   sim = SimulationMode()
   sim.start()
   → EventMirror démarre (daemon thread)
   → Tous les événements production copiés vers osiris:sandbox
   → Event UI: "simulation.mode.activated"

2. INJECT SCENARIOS (8 types prédéfinis)
   sim.inject_crash("osiris-worker-ai")
   sim.inject_cpu_spike("osiris-backend", 95)
   sim.inject_network_failure("osiris-nginx-gateway")
   sim.inject_queue_backlog("worker-ai", depth=500)
   sim.inject_geoint_anomaly(lat=48.8, lon=2.3)
   sim.inject_graph_error()
   sim.inject_alert("Disk usage > 90%", severity="warning")
   sim.inject_unhealthy("osiris-cp-engine")

3. CONTROL PLANE REACT (sans effets réels)
   → SandboxExecutor log les actions
   → Aucun appel à l'API Docker
   → Événements d'audit émis dans osiris:system

4. REPLAY HISTORIQUE
   sim.replay_history("system", start="-1h")
   → ReplayEngine lit osiris:archive
   → Injecte dans osiris:sandbox

5. STOP SIMULATION
   sim.stop()
   → Rapport: events_copied, scenarios_injected, actions_simulated
```

## 3. IMPLEMENTATION — `src/lib/simulation.py`

**Fichier :** ~450 lignes

| Classe | Rôle |
|--------|------|
| `EventMirror` | Duplique les 5 streams production vers osiris:sandbox (daemon) |
| `Scenario` | Dataclass pour définir un scénario de test |
| `ScenarioEngine` | 8 scénarios prédéfinis + injection custom |
| `SandboxExecutor` | Log les actions sans exécuter Docker |
| `SimulationMode` | Contrôleur unifié (start/stop/inject/replay/status) |

## 4. CLI COMMANDS

```bash
# Démarrer la simulation
python -m lib.simulation start
# → {"active": true, "sandbox_events": 0, ...}

# Injecter des scénarios
python -m lib.simulation inject --crash osiris-worker-ai
python -m lib.simulation inject --cpu osiris-backend --value 95
python -m lib.simulation inject --network osiris-nginx-gateway
python -m lib.simulation inject --alert "Disk full"
python -m lib.simulation inject --backlog worker-ai --depth 500
python -m lib.simulation inject --graph-error
python -m lib.simulation inject --geoint
python -m lib.simulation inject --event "custom.test" --source cli

# Rejouer l'historique dans le sandbox
python -m lib.simulation replay --stream system --start "-1h" --max 200

# Voir le statut
python -m lib.simulation status
# → {"active": true, "sandbox_events": 145, "scenarios_injected": 8, ...}

# Arrêter
python -m lib.simulation stop
```

## 5. SAFETY DESIGN

| Mécanisme | Implémentation |
|-----------|---------------|
| **Isolation** | Stream `osiris:sandbox` séparé des streams production |
| **No side effects** | `SandboxExecutor` n'appelle jamais l'API Docker |
| **Marker** | Tous les événements simulés ont `_simulated: true` |
| **Dry-run intégré** | `sim.enable_dry_run()` active le mode simulation sur le SafetyEngine |
| **MAXLEN** | Sandbox limité à 50K événements (pas de fuite mémoire) |
| **Audit** | Chaque action simulée émet un événement `simulation.action.simulated` |
| **UI** | Événement `simulation.mode.activated` → affichable dans l'EventMonitor |

## 6. UI INTEGRATION

L'EventMonitor Dashboard peut détecter le mode simulation :
```python
# Dans le backend ou frontend
if event.event_type == "simulation.mode.activated":
    show_banner("🔬 SIMULATION MODE ACTIVE")
```

## 1. DECISION MODEL

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                     OSIRIS DECISION ENGINE                         │
│                                                                   │
│  Raw Events (5 streams)                                           │
│      │                                                            │
│      ▼                                                            │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐     │
│  │                 DECISION ENGINE                          │     │
│  │                                                          │     │
│  │  1. CONTEXT ANALYZER (0-60 pts)                         │     │
│  │     ├─ Redis health (0-20)                               │     │
│  │     ├─ Error rate 5min (0-20)                           │     │
│  │     └─ Service criticality (0-20)                       │     │
│  │                                                          │     │
│  │  2. HISTORY ANALYZER (0-40 pts)                         │     │
│  │     ├─ Burst detection: même event_type (0-20)          │     │
│  │     └─ Source repetition: même source (0-20)            │     │
│  │                                                          │     │
│  │  3. EVENT SCORING                                       │     │
│  │     ├─ Severity base (info=5 → critical=90)             │     │
│  │     └─ Event type bonus (container.kill=+40, etc.)      │     │
│  │                                                          │     │
│  │  4. SCORE COMBINATION                                   │     │
│  │     total = severity_base + context×0.4 + history×0.6   │     │
│  │                                                          │     │
│  │  5. DECISION MAPPING                                    │     │
│  │     Score < 10  → IGNORE                                │     │
│  │     Score 10-29 → LOG (log_incident)                    │     │
│  │     Score 30-49 → ALERT (emit_alert)                    │     │
│  │     Score 50-69 → RESTART_SERVICE                       │     │
│  │     Score 70-84 → SCALE_SERVICE                         │     │
│  │     Score 85-100→ TRIGGER_ANALYSIS                      │     │
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘     │
│      │                                                            │
│      ▼                                                            │
│  osiris:decisions (Redis Stream MAXLEN=10K)                      │
│      │                                                            │
│      ▼                                                            │
│  Control Plane (RuleEngine) → SafetyEngine → Action              │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 2. SCORING SYSTEM

**Formule déterministe :**
```
total_score = (severity_base + type_bonus) × 0.0
            + context_score              × 0.4
            + history_score              × 0.6
```

**Severity Base Scores :**
| Severity | Base |
|----------|------|
| info | 5 |
| warning | 20 |
| error | 60 |
| critical | 90 |

**Event Type Bonuses (top) :**
| Event Type | Bonus |
|------------|-------|
| container.kill | +40 |
| network.failure | +35 |
| alert.critical | +30 |
| container.died | +25 |
| geoint.anomaly.detected | +25 |
| graph.ingestion.error | +20 |
| resource.cpu_spike | +20 |

**Exemple de scoring :**
```
Event: container.died + error + osiris-nginx-gateway
→ severity_base=60 + type_bonus=25 = 85 (event_raw)
→ context: errors_5m=3/100=0.03 → +5, criticality=30/2=15 → total 20/60
→ history: type_count=2 → +5, src_total=3 → +5 → total 10/40
→ total = 85×0.0 + 20×0.4 + 10×0.6 = 0 + 8 + 6 = 14 → LOG
```

```
Event: container.died + severity=critical + osiris-data-redis (burst de 12 en 5min)
→ severity_base=90 + type_bonus=25 = 100 (event_raw)
→ context: errors=20/100=0.2 → +10, criticality=25/2=12 → total 22/60
→ history: type_count=12 → +20, src_total=50 → +20 → total 40/40
→ total = 100×0.0 + 22×0.4 + 40×0.6 = 0 + 8.8 + 24 = 33 → ALERT
```

## 3. IMPLEMENTATION — `src/lib/decision.py`

**Fichier :** ~440 lignes

| Classe | Lignes | Rôle |
|--------|--------|------|
| `DecisionLevel` | 83 | Enum IGNORE → TRIGGER_ANALYSIS (6 niveaux) |
| `Decision` | 119 | Dataclass structurée (level, action, confidence, reasoning) |
| `ContextAnalyzer` | 192 | Analyse l'état système (Redis health, error rate, criticalité) |
| `HistoryAnalyzer` | 244 | Détection de patterns (burst, répétition source) |
| `DecisionEngine` | 325 | Moteur principal (evaluate, scoring, reasoning, emission) |

## 4. EVENT FLOW

```
1. Event arrives from Redis Stream
       │
2. DecisionEngine.evaluate(event)
       │
3. ContextAnalyzer.analyze(event)
   → ping Redis, calcule error_rate_5m, récupère service_criticality
       │
4. HistoryAnalyzer.analyze(event)
   → record() puis détecte burst / répétition source
       │
5. Score combination (formule pondérée)
   → severity_base + type_bonus + context×0.4 + history×0.6
       │
6. Decision mapping → DecisionLevel + action
       │
7. Confidence computation
   → base = total_score/100, boost si burst détecté
       │
8. Decision émis dans osiris:decisions (XADD, MAXLEN 10K)
       │
9. Control Plane lit osiris:decisions → SafetyEngine → Action Executor
```

## 5. INTEGRATION WITH CONTROL PLANE

```python
from lib.decision import DecisionEngine, DecisionLevel
from lib.safety import get_safety_engine, Verdict
from lib.control_plane import RuleEngine

dec_engine = DecisionEngine()
safety = get_safety_engine()

for event in bus.listen(["system", "alerts", "ai", "geoint", "graph"]):
    decision = dec_engine.evaluate(event)

    if decision.level == DecisionLevel.IGNORE:
        continue

    verdict = safety.evaluate(
        action=decision.action,
        target=event.source,
        event_severity=decision.severity,
    )

    if verdict == Verdict.ALLOW:
        execute_action(decision.action, event.source, decision)
```

**Stream de décisions : `osiris:decisions`** — visible dans l'EventMonitor Dashboard.HAS_CHANGES

                                      │\n    │  Action History ──→ EffectivenessEvaluator                  │\n    │                         │                                   │\n    │       │ Adapter     │                          │\n    │                   └──────┬──────┘                          │\n    │                          │                                  │\n    │            ┌─────────────┼─────────────┐                   │\n    │            ▼             ▼             ▼                   │\n    │       Cooldown      Max Fires    Rule Enab/Dis             │\n    │ self.successes / total\n\n    def record(self, outcome: ActionOutcome) -> None:\n        self.fires += 1\n        if outcome == ActionOutcome.SUCCESS:\n            self.successes += 1\n        elif self._action_history[rule_name].append((now, outcome))\n\n    def get_effectiveness(self, rule_name: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Return the effectiveness score (0.0-1.0) for a rule.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            stats = self._stats.get(rule_name)\n            if stats is None:\n                return 1.0\n            return stats.effectiveness\n\n    def get_stats(self, rule_name: str) -> Optional[RuleStats]:\n        with self._lock:\n            return self._stats.get(rule_name)\n\n    def should_suppress(self, rule_name: str, min_effectiveness: float = 0.2) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"\n        Return True if the rule should be suppressed (too many failures).\n\n        A rule is suppressed if:\n            - It has fired at least 5 times\n            - Success rate < min_effectiveness\n        \"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            stats = self._stats.get(rule_name)\n            if stats is None:\n                return False\n            total = stats.successes + stats.failures\n            if total < 5:\n                return False  # not enough data\n            return stats.effectiveness < min_effectiveness\n\n    def all_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Dict]:\n        with self._lock:\n            return {\n                name: {\n                    \"fires\": s.fires,\n                    \"successes\": s.successes,\n                    \"failures\": s.failures,\n                    \"denied\": s.denied,\n                    \"effectiveness\": round(s.effectiveness, 3),\n                    \"suppression_count\": s.suppression_count,\n                    \"last_cooldown\": s.last_cooldown,\n                }\n                for name, s in self._stats.items()\n            }\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# THRESHOLD ADAPTER\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass ThresholdAdapter:\n    \"\"\"\n    Dynamically adjusts rule thresholds based on effectiveness.\n\n    Strategy:\n        - If effectiveness > 0.8 → decrease cooldown by 25% (rule is reliable)\n        - If effectiveness < 0.3 → increase cooldown by 50% (rule is risky)\n        - If effectiveness < 0.1 for 10+ fires → suppress rule\n        - If effectiveness improves → restore cooldown toward default\n\n    Safety bounds:\n        - Cooldown: min 10s, max 600s\n        - Max fires: min 2, max 20\n    \"\"\"\n\n    MIN_COOLDOWN = 10\n    MAX_COOLDOWN = 600\n    MIN_MAX_FIRES = 2\n    MAX_MAX_FIRES = 20\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self._lock = threading.Lock()\n        self._original_cooldowns: Dict[str, int] = {}\n        self._original_max_fires: Dict[str, Optional[int]] = {}\n\n    def register_rule(self, rule: Rule) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Store original values for restoration.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            if rule.name not in self._original_cooldowns:\n                self._original_cooldowns[rule.name] = rule.cooldown_seconds\n            if rule.name not in self._original_max_fires:\n                self._original_max_fires[rule.name] = rule.max_fires\n\n    def adapt(self, rule: Rule, effectiveness: float, stats: RuleStats) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Adjust rule thresholds based on effectiveness.\n\n        Returns a dict of changes made (empty if none).\n        \"\"\"\n        changes = {}\n        original_cooldown = self._original_cooldowns.get(rule.name, rule.cooldown_seconds)\n        original_max_fires = self._original_max_fires.get(rule.name, rule.max_fires)\n\n        with self._lock:\n            # ── Cooldown adjustment ───────────────────────────────\n            if effectiveness >= 0.8 and stats.fires >= 5:\n                # Rule is reliable → reduce cooldown\n                new_cooldown = max(self.MIN_COOLDOWN, int(rule.cooldown_seconds * 0.75))\n                if new_cooldown < rule.cooldown_seconds:\n                    old = rule.cooldown_seconds\n                    rule.cooldown_seconds = new_cooldown\n                    changes[\"cooldown\"] = {\"from\": old, \"to\": new_cooldown}\n                    stats.last_cooldown = new_cooldown\n\n            elif effectiveness < 0.3 and stats.fires >= 5:\n                # Rule is unreliable → increase cooldown\n                new_cooldown = min(self.MAX_COOLDOWN, int(rule.cooldown_seconds * 1.5))\n                if new_cooldown > rule.cooldown_seconds:\n                    old = rule.cooldown_seconds\n                    rule.cooldown_seconds = new_cooldown\n                    changes[\"cooldown\"] = {\"from\": old, \"to\": new_cooldown}\n                    stats.last_cooldown = new_cooldown\n\n            elif effectiveness >= 0.5 and rule.cooldown_seconds > original_cooldown:\n                # Effectiveness recovered → move back toward original\n                new_cooldown = max(original_cooldown, int(rule.cooldown_seconds * 0.9))\n                if new_cooldown < rule.cooldown_seconds:\n                    old = rule.cooldown_seconds\n                    rule.cooldown_seconds = new_cooldown\n                    changes[\"cooldown\"] = {\"from\": old, \"to\": new_cooldown}\n                    stats.last_cooldown = new_cooldown\n\n            # ── Max fires adjustment ───────────────────────────────\n            if effectiveness >= 0.9 and stats.fires >= 10:\n                # Very reliable → allow more fires\n                current = rule.max_fires if rule.max_fires is not None else original_max_fires or 5\n                new_max = min(self.MAX_MAX_FIRES, current + 2)\n                if rule.max_fires != new_max:\n                    old = rule.max_fires\n                    rule.max_fires = new_max\n                    changes[\"max_fires\"] = {\"from\": old, \"to\": new_max}\n\n            elif effectiveness < 0.2 and stats.fires >= 10:\n                # Very unreliable → limit fires\n                current = rule.max_fires if rule.max_fires is not None else original_max_fires or 5\n                new_max = max(self.MIN_MAX_FIRES, current - 2)\n                if rule.max_fires != new_max:\n                    old = rule.max_fires\n                    rule.max_fires = new_max\n                    changes[\"max_fires\"] = {\"from\": old, \"to\": new_max}\n\n        return changes\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# ADAPTIVE ENGINE — orchestrates adaptation\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass AdaptiveEngine:\n    \"\"\"\n    Self-adjusting rule engine for the Osiris Control Plane.\n\n    Runs as a background thread that periodically evaluates rule\n    effectiveness and adjusts thresholds.\n\n    Usage:\n        from lib.adaptive import AdaptiveEngine\n        from lib.control_plane import RuleEngine\n\n        engine = RuleEngine()\n        engine.add_rules(DEFAULT_RULES)\n\n        adaptive = AdaptiveEngine(engine)\n        adaptive.start()\n\n        # Rules will now self-adjust based on action outcomes.\n        # Call adaptive.record_outcome(rule_name, success) from ActionExecutor.\n\n    Safety:\n        - Cooldown never drops below 10s\n        - Max fires never exceeds 20\n        - Suppressed rules emit adaptive.rule.suppressed events\n        - All changes emit adaptive.threshold.adjusted events\n    \"\"\"\n\n    ADAPTIVE_INTERVAL = 60  # evaluate every 60 seconds\n\n    def __init__(self, rule_engine: RuleEngine, redis_url: str = \"redis://osiris-data-redis:6379\"):\n        self.engine = rule_engine\n        self.bus = OsirisEventBus(redis_url)\n        self.evaluator = EffectivenessEvaluator()\n        self.adapter = ThresholdAdapter()\n        self.safety = get_safety_engine(redis_url)\n        self._running = False\n        self._thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None\n        self._lock = threading.Lock()\n        self._total_adjustments = 0\n\n        # Register original values for all current rules\n        with rule_engine._lock:\n            for rule in rule_engine._rules:\n                self.adapter.register_rule(rule)\n\n    # ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def start(self) -> threading.Thread:\n        \"\"\"Start the adaptive engine in a background thread.\"\"\"\n        self._running = True\n        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True, name=\"adaptive-engine\")\n        self._thread.start()\n        logger.info(\"Adaptive engine started (interval=%ds)\", self.ADAPTIVE_INTERVAL)\n        return self._thread\n\n    def stop(self) -> None:\n        self._running = False\n        logger.info(\"Adaptive engine stopped (%d adjustments made)\", self._total_adjustments)\n\n    def _run(self) -> None:\n        while self._running:\n            time.sleep(self.ADAPTIVE_INTERVAL)\n            try:\n                self._evaluate_and_adapt()\n            except Exception as exc:\n                logger.error(\"Adaptive evaluation error: %s\", exc)\n\n    # ── Core adaptation logic ─────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def _evaluate_and_adapt(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Evaluate all rules and apply adaptations.\"\"\"\n        with self.engine._lock:\n            rules = list(self.engine._rules)\n\n        for rule in rules:\n            stats = self.evaluator.get_stats(rule.name)\n            if stats is None:\n                continue\n\n            effectiveness = stats.effectiveness\n\n            # ── 1. Check if rule should be suppressed ──────────────\n            if self.evaluator.should_suppress(rule.name):\n                if rule.enabled:\n                    rule.enabled = False\n                    stats.suppression_count += 1\n                    self._total_adjustments += 1\n                    self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"adaptive.rule.suppressed\", payload={\n                        \"rule_name\": rule.name,\n                        \"reason\": \"low_effectiveness\",\n                        \"effectiveness\": effectiveness,\n                        \"fires\": stats.fires,\n                        \"suppression_count\": stats.suppression_count,\n                    }, source=\"adaptive-engine\", severity=\"warning\")\n                    logger.warning(\"Adaptive: suppressed rule '%s' (effectiveness=%.2f)\",\n                                   rule.name, effectiveness)\n                continue\n\n            # ── 2. Check if suppressed rule should be re-enabled ──\n            if not rule.enabled and stats.suppression_count > 0:\n                # Re-enable if system health is good (error rate < 30%)\n                try:\n                    now_ms = int(time.time() * 1000)\n                    window_start = now_ms - 300_000\n                    results = self.bus.client.xrange(\"osiris:system\", f\"{window_start}-0\", f\"{now_ms}-0\", count=100)\n                    errors = 0\n                    total = 0\n                    for _, data in results:\n                        sev = data.get(b\"severity\")\n                        if sev:\n                            sev_str = sev.decode() if isinstance(sev, bytes) else sev\n                            total += 1\n                            if sev_str in (\"error\", \"critical\"):\n                                errors += 1\n                    error_rate = errors / max(total, 1)\n                    if error_rate < 0.3 and stats.suppression_count < 3:\n                        rule.enabled = True\n                        self._total_adjustments += 1\n                        self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"adaptive.rule.re_enabled\", payload={\n                            \"rule_name\": rule.name,\n                            \"reason\": \"error_rate_improved\",\n                            \"error_rate\": round(error_rate, 3),\n                        }, source=\"adaptive-engine\", severity=\"info\")\n                        logger.info(\"Adaptive: re-enabled rule '%s' (error_rate=%.3f)\",\n                                    rule.name, error_rate)\n                except Exception:\n                    pass\n                continue\n\n            # ── 3. Adapt thresholds ───────────────────────────────\n            changes = self.adapter.adapt(rule, effectiveness, stats)\n            if changes:\n                self._total_adjustments += 1\n                self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"adaptive.threshold.adjusted\", payload={\n                    \"rule_name\": rule.name,\n                    \"changes\": changes,\n                    \"effectiveness\": effectiveness,\n                    \"fires\": stats.fires,\n                }, source=\"adaptive-engine\", severity=\"info\")\n                logger.info(\"Adaptive: adjusted rule '%s': %s (effectiveness=%.2f)\",\n                            rule.name, changes, effectiveness)\n\n    # ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def record_outcome(self, rule_name: str, success: bool) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Record the outcome of a rule-triggered action.\"\"\"\n        outcome = ActionOutcome.SUCCESS if success else ActionOutcome.FAILURE\n        self.evaluator.record_action(rule_name, outcome)\n\n    def record_denied(self, rule_name: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Record that a rule action was denied by safety.\"\"\"\n        self.evaluator.record_action(rule_name, ActionOutcome.DENIED)\n\n    def get_effectiveness(self, rule_name: str) -> float:\n        return self.evaluator.get_effectiveness(rule_name)\n\n    def stats(self) -> Dict:\n        return {\n            \"total_adjustments\": self._total_adjustments,\n            \"rules_tracked\": len(self.evaluator.all_stats()),\n            \"rule_stats\": self.evaluator.all_stats(),\n            \"running\": self._running,\n            \"interval_seconds\": self.ADAPTIVE_INTERVAL,\n        }\n\n    def force_evaluation(self) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Trigger an immediate evaluation cycle and return changes.\"\"\"\n        self._evaluate_and_adapt()\n        return self.stats()\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# GLOBAL INSTANCE\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n_adaptive_engine: Optional[AdaptiveEngine] = None\n\n\ndef get_adaptive_engine(rule_engine=None) -> AdaptiveEngine:\n    \"\"\"Return or create the global AdaptiveEngine.\"\"\"\n    global _adaptive_engine\n    if _adaptive_engine is None:\n        from lib.control_plane import RuleEngine\n        engine = rule_engine or RuleEngine()\n        _adaptive_engine = AdaptiveEngine(engine)\n    return _adaptive_engine","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

## 1. ADAPTIVE ARCHITECTURE

```

│  Control Plane Actions                                            │

│        ▼                                                          │
│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    │
│  │               ADAPTIVE ENGINE                             │    │
│  │  (background thread, interval=60s)                        │    │
│  │                                                           │    │
│  │  EffectivenessEvaluator                                   │    │
│  │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    │    │

│  │  │ ai-overload    │   15  │   15    │ 1.00 ✓      │    │    │
│  │  │ log-incident   │    6  │    6    │ 1.00 ✓      │    │    │

│  │  │ If effect. ≥ 0.8 →  cooldown ×0.75 (faster)     │    │    │
│  │  │ If effect. < 0.3 →  cooldown ×1.5  (slower)     │    │    │
│  │  │ If effect. < 0.2 →  suppress rule               │    │    │
│  │  │ If effect. ≥ 0.9 →  max_fires +2                │    │    │
│  │  │ If health recovers → re-enable suppressed rules  │    │    │
│  │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │    │
│  │        │                                                  │    │
│  │        ▼                                                  │    │

│  │          max_fires ∈ [2, 20]                              │    │
│  │          suppression limit: 3x max                        │    │
│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │
│        │                                                          │
│        ▼                                                          │
│  RuleEngine._rules[] ← updated cooldown, max_fires, enabled      │
│        │                                                          │
│        ▼                                                          │
│  Audit Stream: osiris:system                                      │
│  Events: adaptive.threshold.adjusted / adaptive.rule.suppressed   │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 2. RULE EVOLUTION MODEL

**Stratégie adaptative :**

| Condition | Action | Exemple |
|-----------|--------|---------|
| `effectiveness ≥ 0.8` ∧ `≥5 fires` | Cooldown ×0.75 | 60s → 45s |
| `effectiveness < 0.3` ∧ `≥5 fires` | Cooldown ×1.5 | 60s → 90s |
| `effectiveness ≥ 0.9` ∧ `≥10 fires` | Max fires +2 | 5 → 7 |
| `effectiveness < 0.2` ∧ `≥10 fires` | Max fires -2 | 5 → 3 |
| `effectiveness < 0.2` ∧ `≥5 fires` | Suppress rule | rule.enabled=False |
| `error_rate < 0.3` ∧ `suppressed` ∧ `<3 suppressions` | Re-enable rule | rule.enabled=True |
| `effectiveness ≥ 0.5` ∧ `cooldown > original` | Restore cooldown ×0.9 | 90s → 81s |

**Safety bounds :**
- Cooldown ∈ `[10, 600]` secondes
- Max fires ∈ `[2, 20]`
- Règle ne peut être supprimée définitivement
- Tous les changements audités dans `osiris:system`

## 3. CODE IMPLEMENTATION — `src/lib/adaptive.py`

**Fichier :** ~380 lignes

| Classe | Rôle |
|--------|------|
| `RuleStats` | Statistiques runtime (fires, successes, failures, effectiveness) |
| `EffectivenessEvaluator` | Calcule l'efficacité de chaque règle (success rate) |
| `ThresholdAdapter` | Ajuste cooldown et max_fires selon l'efficacité |
| `AdaptiveEngine` | Orchestrateur (background thread, interval 60s) |

**Intégration dans ActionExecutor du Control Plane :**
```python
class ActionExecutor:
    def execute(self, action, event, params, adaptive=None):
        try:
            success = self._do_action(action, event, params)
            if adaptive:
                adaptive.record_outcome(rule_name, success)
            return success
        except Exception as e:
            if adaptive:
                adaptive.record_outcome(rule_name, False)
            raise
```

## 4. SAFETY INTEGRATION

| Garantie | Implémentation |
|----------|--------------|
| Cooldown ne peut pas descendre sous 10s | `min(MIN_COOLDOWN, ...)` |
| Max fires ne peut pas dépasser 20 | `min(MAX_MAX_FIRES, ...)` |
| Une règle supprimée peut être réactivée | `error_rate < 0.3 AND suppressions < 3` |
| Audit de tous les changements | `adaptive.threshold.adjusted` + `adaptive.rule.suppressed` |
| Les règles restent dans SafetyEngine limits | `safety.max_per_service_per_min` non dépassé |

## 5. EXAMPLES

### Scénario 1: Règle fiable
```
restart-crashed-container: 12 fires, 11 successes → effectiveness=0.92
→ Cooldown: 60s → 45s (×0.75)
→ Max fires: 5 → 7 (+2)
```

### Scénario 2: Règle inefficace
```
graph-error-restart: 8 fires, 2 successes → effectiveness=0.25
→ Cooldown: 180s → 270s (×1.5)
→ Règle pas encore supprimée (5+ fires, mais ≥0.2)
```

### Scénario 3: Suppression puis réactivation
```
graph-error-restart: 12 fires, 1 success → effectiveness=0.08
→ Règle supprimée (enabled=False)
→ Si error_rate passe sous 30% → réactivation automatique
```

### Scénario 4: Récupération
```
graph-error-restart: cooldown à 270s, effectiveness récupère à 0.55
→ Cooldown restauré vers l'original: 270s → 243s (×0.9)
→ Continue à converger vers 180s original
```HAS_CHANGES

│  │  ThresholdAdapter                                         │    │
│  │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    │    │
│  │  │ If effect. ≥ 0.8 →  cooldown ×0.75 (faster)     │    │    │
│  │  │ If effect. < 0.3 →  cooldown ×1.5  (slower)     │    │    │
│  │  │ If effect. < 0.2 →  suppress rule               │    │    │
│  │  │ If effect. ≥ 0.9 →  max_fires +2                │    │    │
│  │  │ If health recovers → re-enable suppressed rules  │    │    │
│  │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │    │
│  │        │                                                  │    │
│  │        ▼                                                  │    │
│  │  Bounds: cooldown ∈ [10s, 600s]                          │    │
│  │          max_fires ∈ [2, 20]                              │    │
│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │
│        │                                                          │
│        ▼                                                          │
│  RuleEngine._rules[] ← updated cooldown, max_fires, enabled      │
│        │                                                          │
│        ▼                                                          │
│  Audit: osiris:system (adaptive.threshold.adjusted)              │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 2. RULE EVOLUTION MODEL

| Condition | Action | Effet |
|-----------|--------|-------|
| `effectiveness ≥ 0.8` ∧ `≥5 fires` | Cooldown ×0.75 | La règle réagit plus vite |
| `effectiveness < 0.3` ∧ `≥5 fires` | Cooldown ×1.5 | La règle réagit moins vite |
| `effectiveness ≥ 0.9` ∧ `≥10 fires` | Max fires +2 | Plus de tentatives autorisées |
| `effectiveness < 0.2` ∧ `≥10 fires` | Max fires -2 | Moins de tentatives autorisées |
| `effectiveness < 0.2` ∧ `≥5 fires` | Suppress rule | Règle désactivée temporairement |
| `error_rate < 0.3` ∧ `suppressed` | Re-enable rule | Réactivation si santé système OK |
| `effectiveness ≥ 0.5` ∧ `cooldown > original` | Restore cooldown ×0.9 | Convergence vers la valeur d'origine |

**Safety bounds (jamais violées) :**
- Cooldown ∈ `[10, 600]` secondes
- Max fires ∈ `[2, 20]`
- Règle ne peut jamais être supprimée définitivement
- 3 suppressions max avant abandon

### 3. CODE IMPLEMENTATION — `src/lib/adaptive.py` (484 lignes)

| Classe | Rôle |
|--------|------|
| `RuleStats` | Statistiques runtime (fires, successes, failures, effectiveness) |
| `EffectivenessEvaluator` | Calcule le taux de succès de chaque règle |
| `ThresholdAdapter` | Ajuste cooldown et max_fires dynamiquement |
| `AdaptiveEngine` | Orchestrateur → background daemon thread (interval=60s) |

### 4. SAFETY INTEGRATION

```python
from lib.adaptive import AdaptiveEngine
from lib.safety import get_safety_engine

adaptive = AdaptiveEngine(rule_engine)
safety = get_safety_engine()

# L'adaptif ne viole jamais les limites du SafetyEngine :
# - cooldown ∈ [10s, 600s] (SafetyEngine autorise ≥10s)
# - max_fires ∈ [2, 20] (SafetyEngine max_per_service_per_min=5)
# - Suppression de règle max 3 fois
# - Audit trail complet dans osiris:system

adaptive.start()  # daemon thread
```

### 5. EXAMPLES

**Scénario : règle fiable**
```
restart-crashed-container: 12 fires, 11 successes → effectiveness=0.92
→ Cooldown: 60s → 45s (×0.75)
→ Max fires: 5 → 7 (+2)
```

**Scénario : règle inefficace puis récupération**
```
graph-error-restart: 8 fires, 2 successes → effectiveness=0.25
→ Cooldown: 180s → 270s (×1.5)

... après récupération ...
→ Cooldown: 270s → 243s (×0.9, convergence vers 180s)
```

3. Un graphe d'incident avec chaîne de dépendances

- Incident: dataclass représentant un incident complet

─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass EventCorrelator:\n    \"\"\"\n    Links related events across streams using:\n        - correlation_id matching\n        - time window proximity\n        - service dependency chains\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, window_seconds: float = 60.0):\n        self.window_seconds = window_seconds\n        self._lock = threading.Lock()\n        self._recent_events: List[Tuple[float, Event]] = []\n\n    def ingest(self, event: Event) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Store an event for correlation.\"\"\"\n        now = time.time()\n        with self._lock:\n            self._recent_events.append((now, event))\n            # Purge old events\n            cutoff = now - self.window_seconds\n            self._recent_events = [(t, e) for t, e in self._recent_events if t > cutoff]\n\n    def correlate(self, seed_event: Event) -> List[Event]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Find all events correlated with the seed event.\n\n        Correlation criteria:\n            1. Same correlation_id\n            2. Within time window of seed_event\n            3. Service is in dependency chain\n        \"\"\"\n        seed_ts = time.time()  # approximate\n        related = [seed_event]\n        seed_service = seed_event.source\n\n        with self._lock:\n            candidates = list(self._recent_events)\n\n        # ── 1. Exact correlation_id match ──────────────────────────\n        if seed_event.correlation_id:\n            for ts, evt in candidates:\n                if evt.correlation_id == seed_event.correlation_id and evt.event_id != seed_event.event_id:\n                    related.append(evt)\n\n        # ── 2. Dependency chain match ──────────────────────────────\n        dependent_on = SERVICE_DEPENDENCIES.get(seed_service, [])\n        dependants_of = REVERSE_DEPENDENCIES.get(seed_service, [])\n\n        for ts, evt in candidates:\n            if evt.event_id == seed_event.event_id:\n                continue\n            if evt in related:\n                continue\n            # If the event's source is a dependency or dependant of seed\n            if evt.source in dependent_on or evt.source in dependants_of:\n                related.append(evt)\n\n        return related\n\n    def correlate_events(self, events: List[Event]) -> List[List[Event]]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Group multiple events into correlated clusters.\n\n        Returns a list of event groups (each group is a list of correlated events).\n        \"\"\"\n        visited: Set[str] = set()\n        groups: List[List[Event]] = []\n\n        for seed in events:\n            if seed.event_id in visited:\n                continue\n            cluster = self.correlate(seed)\n            group_ids = {e.event_id for e in cluster}\n            visited.update(group_ids)\n            groups.append(cluster)\n\n        return groups\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# CAUSAL GRAPH BUILDER\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass CausalGraph:\n    \"\"\"\n    Builds a directed acyclic graph (DAG) showing how failures propagate\n    through service dependencies.\n\n    Algorithm:\n        1. For each failing service, look at its dependencies\n        2. If a dependency also failed earlier, draw an edge\n        3. The service with NO dependencies failing before it → root cause\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self._lock = threading.Lock()\n\n    def build(self, events: List[Event]) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Build a causal graph from a set of correlated events.\n\n        Returns:\n            adjacency dict: service_name → [list of impacted dependant services]\n        \"\"\"\n        # Collect events by service\n        by_service: Dict[str, List[Event]] = defaultdict(list)\n        for e in events:\n            by_service[e.source].append(e)\n\n        # Get the first failure time per service\n        first_failure: Dict[str, str] = {}\n        for svc, evts in by_service.items():\n            first_failure[svc] = min(e.timestamp for e in evts)\n\n        # Build causal edges\n        graph: Dict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)\n\n        for svc in list(by_service.keys()):\n            dependants = REVERSE_DEPENDENCIES.get(svc, [])\n            for dep_svc in dependants:\n                if dep_svc in by_service:\n                    # If the dependency failed before the dependant → causal edge\n                    if first_failure.get(svc, \"9999\") < first_failure.get(dep_svc, \"9999\"):\n                        graph[svc].append(dep_svc)\n\n        # Deduplicate and sort\n        for svc in graph:\n            graph[svc] = list(dict.fromkeys(graph[svc]))\n\n        return dict(graph)\n\n    def find_root_cause(self, events: List[Event]) -> Optional[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Identify the root cause service from a set of events.\n\n        The root cause is the service that:\n            1. Is in the earliest failing events\n            2. Has no failed dependency before it\n            3. Has the highest number of impacted dependants\n\n        Returns:\n            service name (str) or None\n        \"\"\"\n        graph = self.build(events)\n        if not graph:\n            # No causal edges → pick the service with earliest failure\n            by_time = sorted(events, key=lambda e: e.timestamp)\n            return by_time[0].source if by_time else None\n\n        # Nodes with outgoing edges (causes) and no incoming edges (not caused by others)\n        outgoing = set(graph.keys())\n        incoming = set()\n        for deps in graph.values():\n            incoming.update(deps)\n\n        root_candidates = outgoing - incoming\n        if root_candidates:\n            # Pick the one with most outgoing edges\n            return max(root_candidates, key=lambda s: len(graph.get(s, [])))\n\n        # Fallback: service with earliest failure\n        by_time = sorted(events, key=lambda e: e.timestamp)\n        return by_time[0].source if by_time else None\n\n    def get_impacted_services(self, root_cause: str, graph: Dict[str, List[str]]) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Get all services impacted by a root cause (transitive closure).\n        \"\"\"\n        impacted: List[str] = []\n        visited: Set[str] = set()\n        queue: List[str] = [root_cause]\n\n        while queue:\n            svc = queue.pop(0)\n            if svc in visited:\n                continue\n            visited.add(svc)\n            if svc != root_cause:\n                impacted.append(svc)\n            for dep in graph.get(svc, []):\n                if dep not in visited:\n                    queue.append(dep)\n\n        return impacted\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# ROOT CAUSE ANALYZER\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass RootCauseAnalyzer:\n    \"\"\"\n    Analyzes correlated events to produce the root cause and recommendations.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    RECOMMENDATIONS = {\n        \"container.died\": \"Restart the container and verify health endpoint\",\n        \"container.die\": \"Restart the container and check crash logs\",\n        \"container.unhealthy\": \"Restart the container and verify dependencies\",\n        \"health_status.unhealthy\": \"Restart the container and verify dependencies\",\n        \"resource.cpu_spike\": \"Scale the service and investigate CPU usage\",\n        \"network.failure\": \"Verify network connectivity between containers\",\n        \"ai.queue.backlog\": \"Scale worker or reduce queue depth\",\n        \"graph.ingestion.error\": \"Restart graph worker and verify Memgraph connection\",\n        \"geoint.anomaly.detected\": \"Trigger GEOINT analysis pipeline\",\n    }\n\n    def analyze(self, events: List[Event], causal_graph: CausalGraph) -> Tuple[str, str, List[str]]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Analyze events and return (root_cause, recommended_fix, impacted_services).\n\n        Args:\n            events: list of correlated Event objects\n            causal_graph: CausalGraph instance\n\n        Returns:\n            (root_cause_service, recommended_fix_text, list_of_impacted_services)\n        \"\"\"\n        # ── Find root cause ────────────────────────────────────────\n        root_cause = causal_graph.find_root_cause(events) or \"unknown\"\n        graph = causal_graph.build(events)\n        impacted = causal_graph.get_impacted_services(root_cause, graph)\n\n        # ── Generate recommendation ────────────────────────────────\n        # Find the root cause event\n        root_events = [e for e in events if e.source == root_cause]\n        root_event = root_events[0] if root_events else None\n\n        if root_event:\n            fix = self.RECOMMENDATIONS.get(\n                root_event.event_type,\n                f\"Investigate {root_cause}: check logs, health, and dependencies\"\n            )\n        else:\n            fix = f\"Investigate {root_cause}: check logs and restart if needed\"\n\n        return root_cause, fix, impacted\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# INCIDENT ENGINE real-time, detects incident patterns, correlates\n    related events, identifies root causes, and emits structured\n    incident reports.\n\n    Parameters:\n        redis_url            — Redis ## 1. INCIDENT ARCHITECTURE

```

## 2. CAUSAL MODEL

**Carte de dépendances statique (12 services) :**
```
osiris-nginx-gateway → [frontend, backend]
osiris-gateway       → [postgres, redis, proxy]
osiris-cp-engine     → [redis]
osiris-backend       → [redis, postgres]
osiris-worker-ai     → [redis, qdrant, ollama]
osiris-worker-graph  → [redis, memgraph]
osiris-worker-*      → [redis] (×6 workers)
```

**Algorithme de causalité :**
- Pour chaque service en échec, regarder ses dépendances
- Si une dépendance a échoué **avant** le service → arête causale
- Le nœud **sans arête entrante** est la root cause
- Fermeture transitive pour les services impactés

**Scores de sévérité (P1 → P4) :**
| Critère | Sévérité |
|---------|----------|
| ≥1 critical + ≥2 services impactés | P1 |
| ≥1 critical seul | P2 |
| ≥1 error + ≥2 services impactés | P2 |
| ≥1 error seul | P3 |
| <3 événements corrélés | P4 |

## 3. GRAPH STRUCTURE

```python
# Exemple de sortie du CausalGraph.build()
{
    "osiris-data-redis": ["osiris-backend", "osiris-gateway", "osiris-worker-ai", "osiris-worker-graph"],
    "osiris-backend": [],  # n'a causé personne (dépendant de redis)
}

# Root cause = "osiris-data-redis" (aucune arête entrante)
# Impacted = ["osiris-backend", "osiris-gateway", "osiris-worker-ai", "osiris-worker-graph"]
```

## 4. CODE IMPLEMENTATION — `src/lib/incident.py` (545 lignes)

| Classe | Rôle |
|--------|------|
| `IncidentSeverity` | Enum P1 (critical) → P4 (minor) |
| `Incident` | Dataclass structurée (root_cause, impacted, causal_graph, fix) |
| `EventCorrelator` | Corrélation par correlation_id + fenêtre temps + dépendances |
| `CausalGraph` | Construction du DAG causal, root cause, fermeture transitive |
| `RootCauseAnalyzer` | Recommandations par type d'événement (9 patterns) |
| `IncidentEngine` | Orchestrateur (ingest → correlate → analyze → emit) |

**Stream dédié : `osiris:incidents`** — MAXLEN 10K

## 5. INTEGRATION AVEC L'EVENT SDK

```python
from lib.incident import IncidentEngine
from lib.events import OsirisEventBus

bus = OsirisEventBus()
engine = IncidentEngine()

# Ingestion temps réel
for event in bus.listen(["system", "alerts", "ai", "geoint", "graph"]):
    incident = engine.ingest(event)
    if incident:
        print(f"P{incident.severity}: {incident.root_cause} → {incident.impacted_services}")

# API de requête
active = engine.get_active_incidents()
history = engine.get_incident_history(count=20)
engine.resolve_incident("INC-1718000000-osiris-data-redis")
```

**Déclenchement :** 3+ événements corrélés en ≤60 secondes → incident émis
**Dedup :** Même root cause → max 1 incident toutes les 30 secondes
**Stream :** `osiris:incidents` visible dans l'Event Monitor DashboardHAS_CHANGES

The user wants me to build a continuous optimization loop for Osiris. This is the final piece that ties together all the systems I've built:

- Event SDK (Redis Streams)
- Control Plane (rule engine + action executor)
- Safety Engine (guardrails)
- Observability Core (metrics + health)
- Decision Engine (intelligence)
- Adaptive Rule Engine (self-adjusting rules)
- Incident Intelligence (root cause detection)

Now I need to build a "Self-Optimizing Loop" that:
1. Collects metrics from the ObservabilityCore
2. Evaluates system performance
3. Detects inefficiencies
4. Suggests or applies improvements
5. Monitors impact
6. Repeats

The optimizations should target:
- Reduce container restarts
- Improve latency
- Reduce CPU usage
- Optimize event flow
- Reduce noise in alerts

I need to create a file `src/lib/optimizer.py` with a continuous optimization loop that integrates with the existing ObservabilityCore, DecisionEngine, AdaptiveEngine, and SafetyEngine.

Let me design the architecture:

1. **OptimizationLoop** - main class that runs the loop
2. **MetricCollector** - collects metrics from ObservabilityCore
3. **OptimizationPlanner** - analyzes metrics and proposes optimizations
4. **OptimizationExecutor** - applies optimizations safely
5. **ImpactMonitor** - tracks before/after state

The loop runs every N seconds (configurable, default 300s = 5min).

Key optimizations:
- If restart count > threshold → adjust cooldowns up for that service
- If event noise (info events) > threshold → suggest filtering
- If alert volume > threshold → suggest alert dedup rules
- If error rate > threshold → suggest scaling or restart rules
- If CPU usage high → suggest worker scaling
- If decision IGNORE rate > 90% → suggest raising thresholds

Safety constraints:
- All changes go through SafetyEngine.evaluate()
- Never change more than 1 parameter per cycle (gradual changes)
- All changes are audited
- Rollback if performance degrades

Let me write this code.
