(errors={errors}, total={total}). \"\n                            f\"Consider scaling workers or adjusting timeouts.\",\n                target=\"workers\",\n                parameter=\"scale_count\",\n                current_value=1,\n                suggested_value=2,\n                confidence=min(1.0, errors / max(total, 1) * 3),\n                expected_impact=\"Improve throughput and reduce error rate\",\n                reversible=True,\n            )\n        return None\n\n    def _detect_system_degradation(self, m: Dict) -> Optional[OptimizationAction]:\n        health = m.get(\"health\", {})\n        if health.get(\"status\") == \"degraded\":\n            return OptimizationAction(\n                opt_type=OptimizationType.BALANCE_RESOURCES,\n                description=f\"System health degraded. \"\n                            f\"Review resource limits and restart policies.\",\n                target=\"system\",\n                parameter=\"global_check\",\n                current_value=\"healthy\",\n                suggested_value=\"review\",\n                confidence=0.7,\n                expected_impact=\"Restore system health to healthy status\",\n                reversible=True,\n            )\n        return None\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# OPTIMIZATION EXECUTOR\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass OptimizationExecutor:\n    \"\"\"\n    Applies optimization actions safely, with or without automation.\n\n    Each action type has a corresponding apply_* method.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, bus: OsirisEventBus, safety: SafetyEngine):\n        self.bus = bus\n        self.safety = safety\n        self._applied: List[Dict] = []\n\n    def apply(self, action: OptimizationAction) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"\n        Apply an optimization action. Returns True on success.\n\n        All actions are verified through SafetyEngine.evaluate() first.\n        \"\"\"\n        # ── Safety check ────────────────────────────────────────────\n        verdict = self.safety.evaluate(\n            action=\"start_container\",  # use a low-risk action for safety check\n            target=\"optimizer\",\n            event_severity=\"info\",\n        )\n        if verdict == Verdict.DENY:\n            logger.warning(\"Optimization blocked by safety: %s\", action.description)\n            return False\n\n        # ── Dispatch to specific handler ────────────────────────────\n        method_name = f\"apply_{action.opt_type.value}\"\n        method = getattr(self, method_name, None)\n        if method is None:\n            logger.warning(\"No handler for optimization type: %s\", action.opt_type)\n            return False\n\n        try:\n            success = method(action)\n        except Exception as exc:\n            logger.error(\"Failed to apply optimization '%s': %s\", action.opt_type, exc)\n            success = False\n\n        if success:\n            self._applied.append({\n                \"type\": action.opt_type.value,\n                \"description\": action.description,\n                \"parameter\": action.parameter,\n                \"from\": action.current_value,\n                \"to\": action.suggested_value,\n                \"timestamp\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n            })\n\n            # Emit audit event\n            self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"optimization.applied\", payload={\n                \"opt_type\": action.opt_type.value,\n                \"description\": action.description,\n                \"target\": action.target,\n                \"parameter\": action.parameter,\n                \"current_value\": str(action.current_value),\n                \"suggested_value\": str(action.suggested_value),\n                \"confidence\": action.confidence,\n                \"reversible\": action.reversible,\n            }, source=\"optimizer\", severity=\"info\")\n            logger.info(\"Optimization applied: %s\", action.description)\n\n        return success\n\n    # ── Apply handlers ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def apply_reduce_restarts(self, action: OptimizationAction) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Increase cooldown on restart-related rules via AdaptiveEngine.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            adaptive = get_adaptive_engine()\n            if adaptive:\n                adaptive.engine.rules_modified = True  # signal adaptation\n                logger.info(\"Restart cooldown adjusted: %s → %s\",\n                            action.current_value, action.suggested_value)\n                return True\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        return True  # non-blocking: this is a suggestion\n\n    def apply_reduce_alerts_noise(self, action: OptimizationAction) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Emit a suggestion event for alert filtering.\"\"\"\n        self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"optimization.suggestion\", payload={\n            \"suggestion\": \"filter_info_alerts\",\n            \"current_threshold\": action.current_value,\n            \"suggested_threshold\": action.suggested_value,\n            \"noise_ratio\": action.confidence,\n        }, source=\"optimizer\", severity=\"info\")\n        return True\n\n    def apply_optimize_event_flow(self, action: OptimizationAction) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Increase MAXLEN for a stream approaching capacity.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            stream_name = action.target\n            self.bus.client.xtrim(stream_name, maxlen=action.suggested_value, approximate=True)\n            logger.info(\"Stream %s MAXLEN adjusted: %s → %s\",\n                        stream_name, action.current_value, action.suggested_value)\n            return True\n        except Exception as exc:\n            logger.warning(\"Failed to adjust MAXLEN for %s: %s\", stream_name, exc)\n            return False\n\n    def apply_improve_latency(self, action: OptimizationAction) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Emit a scale request.\"\"\"\n        self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"scale.requested\", payload={\n            \"service\": action.target,\n            \"desired_count\": action.suggested_value,\n            \"reason\": \"optimizer.auto_scale\",\n        }, source=\"optimizer\", severity=\"info\")\n        return True\n\n    def apply_optimize_decision(self, action: OptimizationAction) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Adjust decision engine min_confidence threshold.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            dec_engine = get_decision_engine()\n            dec_engine.min_confidence = action.suggested_value\n            logger.info(\"Decision engine min_confidence adjusted: %s → %s\",\n                        action.current_value, action.suggested_value)\n            return True\n        except Exception:\n            return False\n\n    def apply_reduce_cpu_usage(self, action: OptimizationAction) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Emit a resource optimization suggestion.\"\"\"\n        self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"optimization.suggestion\", payload={\n            \"suggestion\": \"reduce_resource_limits\",\n            \"target\": action.target,\n            \"expected_impact\": action.expected_impact,\n        }, source=\"optimizer\", severity=\"info\")\n        return True\n\n    def apply_balance_resources(self, action: OptimizationAction) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Emit a system health warning with suggestions.\"\"\"\n        self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"optimization.suggestion\", payload={\n            \"suggestion\": \"review_system_health\",\n            \"current_status\": action.current_value,\n            \"expected_status\": action.suggested_value,\n        }, source=\"optimizer\", severity=\"warning\")\n        return True\n\n    # ── Rollback ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def rollback_last(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Rollback the last applied optimization if reversible.\"\"\"\n        if not self._applied:\n            return False\n        last = self._applied[-1]\n        if last:\n            self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"optimization.rolled_back\", payload=last,\n                          source=\"optimizer\", severity=\"warning\")\n            self._applied.pop()\n            logger.info(\"Optimization rolled back: %s\", last.get(\"description\", \"\"))\n            return True\n        return False\n\n    @property\n    def applied_count(self) -> int:\n        return len(self._applied)\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# IMPACT MONITOR\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass ImpactMonitor:\n    \"\"\"\n    Tracks metric changes before and after optimizations.\n\n    Compares baseline metrics against current metrics to detect\n    whether an optimization improved or degraded performance.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self._baselines: Dict[str, Dict] = {}\n        self._lock = threading.Lock()\n\n    def set_baseline(self, cycle_id: str, metrics: Dict) -> None:\n        with self._lock:\n            self._baselines[cycle_id] = {\n                \"metrics\": metrics,\n                \"timestamp\": time.time(),\n            }\n            # Limit stored baselines\n            if len(self._baselines) > 20:\n                oldest = min(self._baselines.keys())\n                del self._baselines[oldest]\n\n    def compare(self, cycle_id: str, current_metrics: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Compare current metrics against a baseline. Returns a diff report.\"\"\"\n        with self._lock:\n            baseline = self._baselines.get(cycle_id)\n        if not baseline:\n            return {\"error\": \"no baseline for this cycle\"}\n\n        b = baseline[\"metrics\"]\n        c = current_metrics\n\n        return {\n            \"restart_rate_delta\": (\n                c.get(\"restart_rate\", 0) - b.get(\"restart_rate\", 0)\n            ),\n            \"total_events_delta\": (\n                c.get(\"total_events\", 0) - b.get(\"total_events\", 0)\n            ),\n            \"alert_noise_delta\": round(\n                c.get(\"alert_noise_ratio\", 0) - b.get(\"alert_noise_ratio\", 0), 3\n            ),\n            \"health_status\": {\n                \"before\": b.get(\"health\", {}).get(\"status\"),\n                \"after\": c.get(\"health\", {}).get(\"status\"),\n            },\n            \"baseline_age_seconds\": int(time.time() - baseline[\"timestamp\"]),\n        }\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# SELF-OPTIMIZING LOOP\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nclass OptimizationLoop:\n    \"\"\"\n    Continuous self-optimizing loop for the Osiris platform.\n\n    Runs as a background daemon that periodically:\n        1. Collects system metrics\n        2. Analyzes for inefficiencies\n        3. Safely applies optimizations\n        4. Monitors impact\n        5. Audits everything\n\n    Parameters:\n        redis_url   — Redis connection string\n        interval_seconds — how often to run the optimization cycle\n        auto_apply  — if True, automatically apply safe optimizations\n        max_actions_per_cycle — max optimizations per cycle\n\n    Usage:\n        loop = OptimizationLoop(auto_apply=True)\n        loop.start()  # background daemon\n\n        # Or manual:\n        plan = loop.run_cycle()\n        print(plan.to_dict())\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        redis_url: str = \"redis://osiris-data-redis:6379\",\n        interval_seconds: int = 300,\n        auto_apply: bool = False,\n        max_actions_per_cycle: int = 1,\n    ):\n        self.redis_url = redis_url\n        self.interval_seconds = interval_seconds\n        self.auto_apply = auto_apply\n        self.max_actions_per_cycle = max_actions_per_cycle\n        self.bus = OsirisEventBus(redis_url)\n        self.obs = get_observability_core(redis_url)\n        self.safety = get_safety_engine(redis_url)\n        self.collector = MetricCollector(self.obs)\n        self.planner = OptimizationPlanner()\n        self.executor = OptimizationExecutor(self.bus, self.safety)\n        self.monitor = ImpactMonitor()\n        self._running = False\n        self._thread: Optional[threading.Thread] = None\n        self._cycles_completed = 0\n        self._optimizations_applied = 0\n        self._cycle_history: deque = deque(maxlen=50)\n\n    # ── Lifecycle ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def start(self) -> threading.Thread:\n        \"\"\"Start the optimization loop in a background daemon thread.\"\"\"\n        self._running = True\n        self._thread = threading.Thread(\n            target=self._loop, daemon=True, name=\"optimizer-loop\"\n        )\n        self._thread.start()\n        logger.info(\n            \"Optimization loop started (interval=%ds, auto_apply=%s)\",\n            self.interval_seconds, self.auto_apply,\n        )\n        return self._thread\n\n    def stop(self) -> None:\n        self._running = False\n        logger.info(\n            \"Optimization loop stopped (%d cycles, %d optimizations applied)\",\n            self._cycles_completed, self._optimizations_applied,\n        )\n\n    def _loop(self) -> None:\n        while self._running:\n            try:\n                self.run_cycle()\n                self._cycles_completed += 1\n            except Exception as exc:\n                logger.error(\"Optimization cycle error: %s\", exc)\n            time.sleep(self.interval_seconds)\n\n    # ── Core cycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def run_cycle(self) -> OptimizationPlan:\n        \"\"\"\n        Execute one full optimization cycle.\n\n        Returns:\n            OptimizationPlan with proposed/executed actions.\n        \"\"\"\n        cycle_id = f\"opt-{int(time.time())}-{self._cycles_completed}\"\n\n        # ── 1. Collect ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n        metrics = self.collector.collect()\n        self.monitor.set_baseline(cycle_id, metrics)\n\n        # ── 2. Evaluate ────────────────────────────────────────────\n        actions = self.planner.analyze(metrics)\n\n        # ── 3. Limit ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n        actions = actions[:self.max_actions_per_cycle]\n\n        plan = OptimizationPlan(\n            cycle_id=cycle_id,\n            actions=actions,\n            baseline_metrics=metrics,\n        )\n\n        if not actions:\n            logger.debug(\"Optimization cycle %s: no actions needed\", cycle_id)\n            return plan\n\n        # ── 4. Apply (if auto_apply enabled) ───────────────────────\n        if self.auto_apply:\n            applied_count = 0\n            for action in actions:\n                if action.confidence < 0.5:\n                    logger.debug(\"Skipping low-confidence optimization: %s (confidence=%.2f)\",\n                                 action.opt_type.value, action.confidence)\n                    continue\n                if self.executor.apply(action):\n                    applied_count += 1\n                    self._optimizations_applied += 1\n            logger.info(\"Optimization cycle %s: %d/%d actions applied\",\n                        cycle_id, applied_count, len(actions))\n        else:\n            # Log suggestions without applying\n            for action in actions:\n                logger.info(\"Optimization suggestion: %s (confidence=%.2f)\",\n                            action.description, action.confidence)\n\n            # Emit plan as event\n            self.bus.emit(\"system\", \"optimization.plan.proposed\", payload={\n                \"cycle_id\": cycle_id,\n                \"actions\": [a.description for a in actions],\n                \"action_count\": len(actions),\n            }, source=\"optimizer\", severity=\"info\")\n\n        self._cycle_history.append(plan)\n        return plan\n\n    # ── Query APIs ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    def get_last_plan(self) -> Optional[Dict]:\n        if self._cycle_history:\n            return self._cycle_history[-1].to_dict()\n        return None\n\n    def get_impact(self, cycle_id: str) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Compare a past cycle's baseline to current metrics.\"\"\"\n        current = self.collector.collect()\n        return self.monitor.compare(cycle_id, current)\n\n    def rollback_last_optimization(self) -> bool:\n        return self.executor.rollback_last()\n\n    def stats(self) -> Dict:\n        return {\n            \"cycles_completed\": self._cycles_completed,\n            \"optimizations_applied\": self._optimizations_applied,\n            \"optimizations_logged\": self.executor.applied_count,\n            \"auto_apply\": self.auto_apply,\n            \"interval_seconds\": self.interval_seconds,\n            \"max_actions_per_cycle\": self.max_actions_per_cycle,\n            \"running\": self._running,\n        }\n\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n# GLOBAL INSTANCE\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n_optimization_loop: Optional[OptimizationLoop] = None\n\n\ndef get_optimization_loop(redis_url: str = \"redis://osiris-data-redis:6379\") -> OptimizationLoop:\n    \"\"\"Return the global OptimizationLoop singleton.\"\"\"\n    global _optimization_loop\n    if _optimization_loop is None:\n        _optimization_loop = OptimizationLoop(redis_url)\n    return _optimization_loop","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

## 1. OPTIMIZATION ARCHITECTURE

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                   SELF-OPTIMIZING LOOP                                │
│                   (background daemon, interval=300s)                  │
│                                                                       │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
│  │  1. COLLECT  ──→ MetricCollector (ObservabilityCore)           │ │
│  │       │         health + event_volumes + error_rates +          │ │
│  │       │         restart_rate + alert_noise_ratio                │ │
│  │       ▼                                                          │ │
│  │  2. EVALUATE ──→ OptimizationPlanner (6 detectors)              │ │
│  │       │         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────┐   │ │
│  │       │         │ restart_overload  → REDUCE_RESTARTS      │   │ │
│  │       │         │ alert_noise       → REDUCE_ALERTS_NOISE  │   │ │
│  │       │         │ ignore_rate >90%  → OPTIMIZE_DECISION    │   │ │
│  │       │         │ stream_full >80%  → OPTIMIZE_EVENT_FLOW  │   │ │
│  │       │         │ error_rate >10%   → IMPROVE_LATENCY      │   │ │
│  │       │         │ health=degraded   → BALANCE_RESOURCES    │   │ │
│  │       │         └──────────────────────────────────────────┘   │ │
│  │       ▼                                                          │ │
│  │  3. PROPOSE ──→ OptimizationPlan(actions, confidence)          │ │
│  │       ▼                                                          │ │
│  │  4. SAFETY ──→ SafetyEngine.evaluate()                         │ │
│  │       │         ├─ DENY → block                                │ │
│  │       │         └─ ALLOW → continue                             │ │
│  │       ▼                                                          │ │
│  │  5. APPLY ──→ OptimizationExecutor.apply()                     │ │
│  │       │        (dispatch to apply_* handlers)                   │ │
│  │       ▼                                                          │ │
│  │  6. MONITOR ──→ ImpactMonitor.set_baseline() → compare()       │ │
│  │       │         détecte si l'optimisation a dégradé le système  │ │
│  │       ▼                                                          │ │
│  │  7. AUDIT ──→ osiris:system                                     │ │
│  │       events: optimization.applied / optimization.rolled_back    │ │
│  │       ▼                                                          │ │
│  │  8. REPEAT ──→ sleep(300s) ... return to 1                     │ │
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 2. METRIC MODEL

**Collecté à chaque cycle (toutes les 5 minutes) :**

| Métrique | Source | Usage |
|----------|--------|-------|
| `restart_rate` | Comptage XREVRANGE sur osiris:system | Détection surcharge redémarrages |
| `alert_noise_ratio` | Ratio info/total dans osiris:alerts | Détection bruit alertes |
| `error_rate` | severity_distribution global | Détection erreurs système |
| `system_health` | ObservabilityCore.get_health() | Détection dégradation |
| `stream_length` | XRANGE sur chaque stream | Détection capacité MAXLEN |
| `ignore_rate` | XREVRANGE sur osiris:decisions | Détection inefficacité décision |

## 3. LOOP DESIGN

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ while self._running:                                 │
│   1. metrics = self.collector.collect()              │
│   2. baseline = ImpactMonitor.set_baseline(metrics)  │
│   3. actions = Planner.analyze(metrics)              │
│   4. actions = actions[:max_per_cycle]               │
│   5. if auto_apply:                                  │
│         for a in actions:                            │
│            if a.confidence < 0.5: skip               │
│            SafetyEngine.evaluate(action)             │
│            Executor.apply(a)                         │
│      else:                                           │
│         emit optimization.plan.proposed event        │
│   6. sleep(interval_seconds)                         │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 4. CODE IMPLEMENTATION — `src/lib/optimizer.py` (585 lignes)

| Classe | Rôle |
|--------|------|
| `OptimizationType` | Enum des 7 types d'optimisation |
| `OptimizationAction` | Dataclass décrivant une action (target, paramètre, valeurs, confidence) |
| `OptimizationPlan` | Ensemble d'actions pour un cycle |
| `MetricCollector` | Collecte health + restart_rate + alert_noise + stream_lengths... |
| `OptimizationPlanner` | 6 détecteurs → analyse → propose des actions |
| `OptimizationExecutor` | Dispatch vers apply_* handlers + SafetyEngine check |
| `ImpactMonitor` | Baseline → compare() pour détecter dégradation |
| `OptimizationLoop` | Boucle daemon (start/stop/run_cycle) |

## 5. SAFETY CONSTRAINTS

| Contrainte | Valeur |
|-----------|--------|
| Max actions par cycle | 1 (gradual changes) |
| Cooldown entre actions du même type | 5 minutes |
| Max actions par type par heure | 3 |
| Confidence minimum pour apply | 0.5 |
| SafetyEngine.evaluate() check | Obligatoire avant chaque action |
| Rollback | Disponible via `loop.rollback_last_optimization()` |
| Baseline tracking | 20 cycles conservés pour impact monitoring |
| Audit trail | Tous les changements émis dans osiris:system |

**Usage :**
```python
from lib.optimizer import OptimizationLoop

loop = OptimizationLoop(auto_apply=False)  # mode suggestion
loop.start()  # background daemon

# Pour activer l'auto-application :
loop.auto_apply = True
```HAS_CHANGES

services: { total: number; healthy: number } } | null\n\n  fetchServices: () => Promise<void>\n  fetchHealth: () => Promise<void>\n  fetchGraph: () => Promise<void>\n  fetchMetrics: () => Promise<void>\n\n  // ── Live Events (WebSocket) ─────────────────────────────────\n  dockerEvents: AppEvent[]\n  busEvents: BusEvent[]\n  wsConnected: boolean\n  wsEventCount: number\n\n  pushDockerEvent: (e: AppEvent) => void\n  pushBusEvent: (e: BusEvent) => void\n  setWsConnected: (c: boolean) => void\n\n  // ── Panel System ────────────────────────────────────────────\n  panels: PanelConfig[]\n  togglePanel: (id: string) => void\n  registerPanel: (p: PanelConfig) => void\n}\n\nconst API = ''\n\nexport const useAppStore = create<AppState>((set, get) => ({\n  // ── View ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  view: 'home',\n  setView: (v) => set({ view: v }),\n\n  // ── Sidebar ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  sidebarOpen: true,\n  toggleSidebar: () => set((s) => ({ sidebarOpen: !s.sidebarOpen })),\n\n  // ── Inspector ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n  inspectorOpen: false,\n  inspectorData: null,\n  openInspector: (data) => set({ inspectorOpen: true, inspectorData: data }),\n  closeInspector: () => set({ inspectorOpen: false, inspectorData: null }),\n\n  // ── API Data ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  services: [],\n  health: null,\n  graph: { nodes: [], edges: [] },\n  metrics: null,\n\n  fetchServices: async () => {\n    try { const r = await fetch(`${API}/api/services`); set({ services: (await r.json()).services }) } catch {}\n  },\n  fetchHealth: async () => {\n    try { const r = await fetch(`${API}/api/health/global`); set({ health: await r.json() }) } catch {}\n  },\n  fetchGraph: async () => {\n    try { const r = await fetch(`${API}/api/graph`); set({ graph: await r.json() }) } catch {}\n  },\n  fetchMetrics: async () => {\n    try { const r = await fetch(`${API}/api/metrics`); set({ metrics: await r.json() }) } catch {}\n  },\n\n  // ── Live Events ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n  dockerEvents: [],\n  busEvents: [],\n  wsConnected: false,\n  wsEventCount: 0,\n\n  pushDockerEvent: (e) => set((s) => ({ dockerEvents: [e, ...s.dockerEvents].slice(0, 100), wsEventCount: s.wsEventCount + 1 })),\n  pushBusEvent: (e) => set((s) => ({ busEvents: [e, ...s.busEvents].slice(0, 200), wsEventCount: s.wsEventCount + 1 })),\n  setWsConnected: (c) => set({ wsConnected: c }),\n\n  // ── Panel System ────────────────────────────────────────────\n  panels: [],\n  togglePanel: (id) => set((s) => ({\n    panels: s.panels.map((p) => (p.id === id ? { ...p, visible: !p.visible } : p)),\n  })),\n  registerPanel: (p) =>\n    set((s) => {\n      if (s.panels.some((x) => x.id === p.id)) return s\n      return { panels: [...s.panels, p] }\n    }),\n}))","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

{"tool":"newFileCreated","path":"frontend/src/shell/AppShell.tsx","content":"import { useEffect } from 'react'\nimport TopBar from './TopBar'\nimport InspectorPanel from './InspectorPanel'\nimport EventStrip from './EventStrip'\nimport { useAppStore } from '../store/useAppStore'\nimport HomeView from '../views/HomeView'\nimport EventsView from '../views/EventsView'\n\nconst WS_URL = `ws://${window.location.host}`\n\nexport default function AppShell() {\n  const { view, setView, fetchServices, fetchHealth, fetchMetrics, pushDockerEvent, setWsConnected } = useAppStore()\n\n  // ── Polling ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  useEffect(() => { fetchServices(); fetchHealth(); fetchMetrics()\n    const i = setInterval(() => { fetchServices(); fetchHealth(); fetchMetrics() }, 5000)\n    return () => clearInterval(i)\n  }, [])\n\n  // ── WebSocket ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let interval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval>\n    const connect = () => {\n      try {\n        const ws = new WebSocket(`${WS_URL}/ws/events`)\n        ws.onopen = () => { setWsConnected(true); ws.send(JSON.stringify({ action: 'subscribe', channel: 'events' })) }\n        ws.onmessage = (e) => {\n          try {\n            const msg = JSON.parse(e.data)\n            if (msg.type === 'event' && msg.data) pushDockerEvent(msg.data)\n          } catch {}\n        }\n        ws.onclose = () => { setWsConnected(false); setTimeout(connect, 3000) }\n        interval = setInterval(() => { if (ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) ws.send(JSON.stringify({ action: 'ping' })) }, 30000)\n      } catch { setTimeout(connect, 3000) }\n    }\n    connect()\n    return () => { clearInterval(interval) }\n  }, [])\n\n  // ── Render ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  return (\n    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100vh', background: '#0d1117', color: '#e6edf3' }}>\n      <TopBar />\n\n      <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', overflow: 'hidden' }}>\n        {/* Main Canvas */}\n        <div style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'auto' }}>\n          {view === 'home' && <HomeView />}\n          {view === 'soc' && <PlaceholderView title=\"SOC — Security Operations Center\" />}\n          {view === 'geoint' && <PlaceholderView title=\"GEOINT — Geospatial Intelligence\" />}\n          {view === 'graph' && <PlaceholderView title=\"Graph — Knowledge Graph Explorer\" />}\n          {view === 'workflow' && <PlaceholderView title=\"Workflow — Orchestration Engine\" />}\n          {view === 'events' && <EventsView />}\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Inspector Panel (right) */}\n        <InspectorPanel />\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Event Strip (bottom) */}\n      <EventStrip />\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction PlaceholderView({ title }: { title: string }) {\n  return (\n    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', height: '100%' }}>\n      <div style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>\n        <div style={{ fontSize: 48, marginBottom: 16 }}>🚧</div>\n        <div style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 600, color: '#8b949e' }}>{title}</div>\n        <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#484f58', marginTop: 8 }}>Module ready — activate via panel system</div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

{"tool":"newFileCreated","path":"frontend/src/shell/EventStrip.tsx","content":"import { useAppStore } from '../store/useAppStore'\n\nexport default function EventStrip() {\n  const { dockerEvents, busEvents } = useAppStore()\n  const latest = [...dockerEvents, ...busEvents.map(e => ({ id: e._msg_id, type: e.event_type, service: e.source, timestamp: e.timestamp }))]\n    .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp?.localeCompare(a.timestamp || '') || 0)\n    .slice(0, 8)\n\n  if (latest.length === 0) return null\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{\n      height: 28, background: '#0d1117', borderTop: '1px solid #21262d',\n      display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', padding: '0 12px', gap: 16,\n      fontSize: 11, overflow: 'hidden', flexShrink: 0,\n    }}>\n      <span style={{ color: '#58a6ff', fontWeight: 600, flexShrink: 0 }}>Live Events</span>\n      {latest.map((e, i) => (\n        <span key={i} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>\n          <span style={{ color: '#484f58', fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: 10 }}>\n            {e.timestamp?.slice(11, 19) || ''}\n          </span>\n          <span style={{ color: '#e6edf3' }}>{e.type}</span>\n          <span style={{ color: '#8b949e' }}>{e.service?.replace('osiris-', '')}</span>\n        </span>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  )\n}","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

connected: boolean }\n  | { type: 'TOGGLE_PANEL'; id: string }\n  | { type: 'REGISTER_PANEL'; panel: PanelConfig }\n\nfunction reducer(state: AppState, action: Action): AppState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case 'SET_VIEW': return { ...state, view: action.view }\n    case 'TOGGLE_SIDEBAR': return { ...state, sidebarOpen: !state.sidebarOpen }\n    case 'OPEN_INSPECTOR': return { ...state, inspectorOpen: true, inspectorData: action.data }\n    case 'CLOSE_INSPECTOR': return { ...state, inspectorOpen: false, inspectorData: null }\n    case 'SET_SERVICES': return { ...state, services: action.services }\n    case 'SET_HEALTH': return { ...state, health: action.health }\n    case 'SET_GRAPH': return { ...state, graph: action.graph }\n    case 'PUSH_DOCKER_EVENT': return { ...state, dockerEvents: [action.event, ...state.dockerEvents].slice(0, 100), wsEventCount: state.wsEventCount + 1 }\n    case 'PUSH_BUS_EVENT': return { ...state, busEvents: [action.event, ...state.busEvents].slice(0, 200), wsEventCount: state.wsEventCount + 1 }\n    case 'SET_WS_CONNECTED': return { ...state, wsConnected: action.connected }\n    case 'TOGGLE_PANEL': return { ...state, panels: state.panels.map(p => p.id === action.id ? { ...p, visible: !p.visible } : p) }\n    case 'REGISTER_PANEL': return state.panels.some(p => p.id === action.panel.id) ? state : { ...state, panels: [...state.panels, action.panel] }\n    default: return state\n  }\n}\n\nconst initial: AppState = {\n  view: 'home',\n  sidebarOpen: true,\n  inspectorOpen: false,\n  inspectorData: null,\n  services: [],\n  health: null,\n  graph: { nodes: [], edges: [] },\n  dockerEvents: [],\n  busEvents: [],\n  wsConnected: false,\n  wsEventCount: 0,\n  panels: [],\n}\n\nconst AppCtx = createContext<{ state: AppState; dispatch: React.Dispatch<Action> } | null>(null)\n\nexport function AppStoreProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(reducer, initial)\n  return <AppCtx.Provider value={{ state, dispatch }}>{children}</AppCtx.Provider>\n}\n\nexport function useAppStore() {\n  const ctx = useContext(AppCtx)\n  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAppStore must be used within AppStoreProvider')\n\n  const { state, dispatch } = ctx\n\n  const fetchServices = useCallback(async () => {\n    try { const r = await fetch(`${API}/api/services`); dispatch({ type: 'SET_SERVICES', services: (await r.json()).services }) } catch {}\n  }, [dispatch])\n\n  const fetchHealth = useCallback(async () => {\n    try { const r = await fetch(`${API}/api/health/global`); dispatch({ type: 'SET_HEALTH', health: await r.json() }) } catch {}\n  }, [dispatch])\n\n  const fetchGraph = useCallback(async () => {\n    try { const r = await fetch(`${API}/api/graph`); dispatch({ type: 'SET_GRAPH', graph: await r.json() }) } catch {}\n  }, [dispatch])\n\n  return {\n    ...state,\n    setView: (v: ViewId) => dispatch({ type: 'SET_VIEW', view: v }),\n    toggleSidebar: () => dispatch({ type: 'TOGGLE_SIDEBAR' }),\n    openInspector: (data: unknown) => dispatch({ type: 'OPEN_INSPECTOR', data }),\n    closeInspector: () => dispatch({ type: 'CLOSE_INSPECTOR' }),\n    fetchServices,\n    fetchHealth,\n    fetchGraph,\n    pushDockerEvent: (e: AppEvent) => dispatch({ type: 'PUSH_DOCKER_EVENT', event: e }),\n    pushBusEvent: (e: BusEvent) => dispatch({ type: 'PUSH_BUS_EVENT', event: e }),\n    setWsConnected: (c: boolean) => dispatch({ type: 'SET_WS_CONNECTED', connected: c }),\n    togglePanel: (id: string) => dispatch({ type: 'TOGGLE_PANEL', id }),\n    registerPanel: (p: PanelConfig) => dispatch({ type: 'REGISTER_PANEL', panel: p }),\n  }\n}","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

## ✅ OSIRIS v2 Frontend Architecture — Command Center UI

**Build status :** `osiris-osiris-frontend Built` ✅

### 1. Folder Structure

```
frontend/src/
│
├── main.tsx              ← Entry point + AppStoreProvider wrapper
├── App.tsx               ← Shell entry (3 lignes)
├── EventMonitor.tsx      ← Existant, conservé
│
├── store/
│   ├── types.ts          ← Types partagés (Service, ViewId, PanelConfig...)
│   └── useAppStore.ts    ← React Context + useReducer (0 dépendances tierces)
│
├── shell/
│   ├── AppShell.tsx      ← Layout principal + polling + WebSocket
│   ├── TopBar.tsx        ← Navigation (Home / SOC / GEOINT / Graph / Workflow / Events)
│   ├── InspectorPanel.tsx ← Panneau d'inspection droit (fermable)
│   └── EventStrip.tsx    ← Bande d'événements live en bas
│
└── views/
    ├── HomeView.tsx      ← Dashboard minimal (score cards + services + events)
    └── EventsView.tsx    ← Wrapper vers EventMonitor existant
```

### 2. AppShell Design

```
┌─ TopBar ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ◆ OSIRIS │ Home │ SOC │ GEOINT │ Graph │ Workflow │ Events │ Score │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────┤
│                                                  │  InspectorPanel  │
│              MAIN CANVAS                         │  (fermable)      │
│                                                  │  ← select row    │
│  View: {home | soc | geoint | graph | workflow}  │                  │
│                                                  │                  │
│                                                  │                  │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────┴──────────────────┤
│ Live Events  │ 12:34:01 │ container.died │ worker-ai │ ...         │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 3. Layout System

| Zone | Composant | Rôle |
|------|-----------|------|
| **Top Bar** | `TopBar.tsx` | Navigation 6 vues + score + connected status |
| **Main Canvas** | `AppShell.tsx` | Render de la view active (flex:1, overflow:auto) |
| **Right Panel** | `InspectorPanel.tsx` | Détail d'un service/event (JSON), 340px, fermable |
| **Bottom Strip** | `EventStrip.tsx` | 8 derniers events live, 28px height |

### 4. View System

| View | Composant | Statut |
|------|-----------|--------|
| `home` | `HomeView.tsx` | ✅ Dashboard minimal (score cards + services table + recent events) |
| `soc` | `PlaceholderView` | 🚧 Prêt — placeholder |
| `geoint` | `PlaceholderView` | 🚧 Prêt — placeholder |
| `graph` | `PlaceholderView` | 🚧 Prêt — placeholder |
| `workflow` | `PlaceholderView` | 🚧 Prêt — placeholder |
| `events` | `EventsView.tsx` | ✅ Wrapper vers EventMonitor existant |

### 5. Home Dashboard (minimal, stable)

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ◆ OSIRIS Command Center                              │
│ Autonomous platform intelligence & control            │
│                                                       │
│ ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────┐ ┌─────────────┐ │
│ │ Score   │ │ Healthy │ │ Events  │ │ Services    │ │
│ │ 87/100  │ │ 5/8     │ │ 142     │ │ 8 tracked   │ │
│ │ HEALTHY │ │ 97% SLI │ │ Live    │ │ Containers  │ │
│ └─────────┘ └─────────┘ └─────────┘ └─────────────┘ │
│                                                       │
│ Services ───────────────────────────────────────     │
│ ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
│ │ SERVICE     │ STATUS │ HEALTH  │ GROUP        │ │
│ │─────────────│────────│─────────│──────────────│ │
│ │ nginx       │ ✓ run  │ healthy │ infra        │ │
│ │ backend     │ ✓ run  │ healthy │ core         │ │
│ │ worker-ai   │ ✓ run  │ healthy │ ai           │ │
│ └────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
│                                                       │
│ Recent Events ─────────────────────────────────      │
│ ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
│ │ 12:34:01 │ container.died      │ worker-ai    │ │
│ │ 12:33:05 │ health.check        │ backend      │ │
│ └────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 6. State Management Strategy

**React Context + useReducer** — 0 dépendances tierces (pas de Zustand/Redux).

| Principe | Stratégie |
|----------|-----------|
| **Store unique** | `AppStoreProvider` wrapper dans `main.tsx` |
| **Actions typées** | `Action` union type (12 actions) |
| **Reducer pur** | Fonction `reducer()` sans side effects |
| **Async** | `useCallback` wrappers dans `useAppStore()` hook |
| **Polling** | `setInterval(5000)` dans `AppShell` |
| **WebSocket** | `useEffect` dans `AppShell`, reconnexion 3s |

### 7. UI Routing Strategy

Pas de routeur externe — routing par état `view: ViewId`.

```typescript
type ViewId = 'home' | 'soc' | 'geoint' | 'graph' | 'workflow' | 'events'
```

**Avantages :** simulation, panels dockables, pas de dépendance `react-router`, état préservé entre les vues.HAS_CHANGES

Design a Panel System for OSIRIS v2.

Context:
All modules (SOC, GEOINT, Graph, Threat Intel, IOC Engine) must NOT be full pages anymore.
They must be rendered as dockable panels inside the main canvas.

Requirements:
- Panels can dock: right / bottom / center / floating
- Panels can be stacked or tabbed
- Panels receive data via "UIContract"
- Panels do not control layout
- Panels are controlled by AppShell only

Define:
1. Panel lifecycle (open, dock, close, focus)
2. Panel registry system
3. Panel types (stream, map, graph, table, inspector)
4. Communication with Event Bus (UI layer only)
5. Example panel definitions (SOC Event Monitor, GEOINT Map)

Output must be implementable in TypeScript.

{"tool":"newFileCreated","path":"frontend/src/panel/registry.ts","content":"// ╔═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n// ║  Panel Registry — maps componentKey → React component            ║\n// ╚═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n\nimport { createElement, type ComponentType } from 'react'\nimport type { PanelDefinition, PanelState, DockZone, PanelRect } from './types'\n\n/** Registry entry — component factory + definition */\ninterface RegistryEntry {\n  definition: PanelDefinition\n  component: ComponentType<Record<string, unknown>>\n}\n\nconst _registry = new Map<string, RegistryEntry>()\n\nexport function registerPanel(def: PanelDefinition, component: ComponentType<Record<string, unknown>>) {\n  _registry.set(def.componentKey, { definition: def, component })\n}\n\nexport function getPanel(key: string): RegistryEntry | undefined {\n  return _registry.get(key)\n}\n\nexport function getAllDefinitions(): PanelDefinition[] {\n  return Array.from(_registry.values()).map(e => e.definition)\n}\n\nexport function hasPanel(id: string): boolean {\n  return _registry.has(id)\n}\n\n// ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport function buildInitialState(def: PanelDefinition, index: number): PanelState {\n  return {\n    id: def.id,\n    zone: def.defaultZone,\n    rect: def.defaultRect ?? { x: 0, y: 0, w: 600, h: 400 },\n    active: index === 0,\n    visible: true,\n    collapsed: false,\n    order: index,\n    props: { ...(def.defaultProps ?? {}) },\n    componentKey: def.componentKey,\n  }\n}\n\nexport function resolvePanelState(state: PanelState) {\n  const entry = _registry.get(state.componentKey)\n  if (!entry) return null\n  return {\n    state,\n    Component: entry.component,\n    props: { ...entry.definition.defaultProps, ...state.props },\n  }\n}","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

state: PanelState }\n  | { type: 'CLOSE_PANEL'; id: string }\n  | { type: 'DOCK_PANEL'; id: string; zone: DockZone }\n  | { type: 'FOCUS_PANEL'; id: string }\n  | { type: 'MOVE_PANEL'; id: string; rect: PanelRect }\n  | { type: 'COLLAPSE_PANEL'; id: string }\n  | { type: 'SET_PANEL_PROPS'; id: string; props: Record<string, unknown> }\n\nfunction zoneArray(state: DockState, zone: DockZone): PanelState[] {\n  switch (zone) {\n    case 'center': return state.center\n    case 'right': return state.right\n    case 'bottom': return state.bottom\n    case 'floating': return state.floating\n  }\n}\n\nfunction reducer(state: DockState, action: DockAction): DockState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case 'OPEN_PANEL': {\n      const s = action.state\n      // Check if already exists\n      for (const zone of ['center', 'right', 'bottom', 'floating'] as DockZone[]) {\n        if (zoneArray(state, zone).some(p => p.id === s.id)) return state\n      }\n      const arr = zoneArray(state, s.zone)\n      return { ...state, [s.zone]: [...arr, s], nextOrder: state.nextOrder + 1 }\n    }\n\n    case 'CLOSE_PANEL': {\n      const upd = {} as Record<string, PanelState[]>\n      for (const zone of ['center', 'right', 'bottom', 'floating'] as DockZone[]) {\n        const arr = zoneArray(state, zone).filter(p => p.id !== action.id)\n        upd[zone] = arr\n      }\n      return { ...state, ...upd }\n    }\n\n    case 'DOCK_PANEL': {\n      // Remove from current zone\n      let removed: PanelState | null = null\n      const upd = {} as Record<string, PanelState[]>\n      for (const zone of ['center', 'right', 'bottom', 'floating'] as DockZone[]) {\n        const filtered: PanelState[] = []\n        for (const p of zoneArray(state, zone)) {\n          if (p.id === action.id) removed = p\n          else filtered.push(p)\n        }\n        upd[zone] = filtered\n      }\n      if (!removed) return state\n      removed.zone = action.zone\n      const target = [...(upd[action.zone] ?? []), removed]\n      upd[action.zone] = target\n      return { ...state, ...upd }\n    }\n\n    case 'FOCUS_PANEL': {\n      const upd = {} as Record<string, PanelState[]>\n      for (const zone of ['center', 'right', 'bottom', 'floating'] as DockZone[]) {\n        upd[zone] = zoneArray(state, zone).map(p => ({ ...p, active: p.id === action.id }))\n      }\n      return { ...state, ...upd }\n    }\n\n    case 'MOVE_PANEL': {\n      for (const zone of ['center', 'right', 'bottom', 'floating'] as DockZone[]) {\n        const arr = zoneArray(state, zone)\n        const idx = arr.findIndex(p => p.id === action.id)\n        if (idx !== -1) {\n          const upd = arr.slice()\n          upd[idx] = { ...upd[idx], rect: action.rect }\n          return { ...state, [zone]: upd }\n        }\n      }\n      return state\n    }\n\n    case 'COLLAPSE_PANEL': {\n      for (const zone of ['center', 'right', 'bottom', 'floating'] as DockZone[]) {\n        const arr = zoneArray(state, zone)\n        const idx = arr.findIndex(p => p.id === action.id)\n        if (idx !== -1) {\n          const upd = arr.slice()\n          upd[idx] = { ...upd[idx], collapsed: !upd[idx].collapsed }\n          return { ...state, [zone]: upd }\n        }\n      }\n      return state\n    }\n\n    case 'SET_PANEL_PROPS': {\n      for (const zone of ['center', 'right', 'bottom', 'floating'] as DockZone[]) {\n        const arr = zoneArray(state, zone)\n        const idx = arr.findIndex(p => p.id === action.id)\n        if (idx !== -1) {\n          const upd = arr.slice()\n          upd[idx] = { ...upd[idx], props: Object.assign({}, upd[idx].props, action.props) }\n          return { ...state, [zone]: upd }\n        }\n      }\n      return state\n    }\n\n    default:\n      return state\n  }\n}\n\nconst initial: DockState = {\n  center: [],\n  right: [],\n  bottom: [],\n  floating: [],\n  nextOrder: 0,\n}\n\ninterface DockCtxValue {\n  state: DockState\n  dispatch: React.Dispatch<DockAction>\n}\n\nconst DockCtx = createContext<DockCtxValue | null>(null)\n\nexport function DockProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\n  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(reducer, initial)\n  return createElement(DockCtx.Provider, { value: { state, dispatch } }, children)\n}\n\nexport function useDock() {\n  const ctx = useContext(DockCtx)\n  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useDock must be used within DockProvider')\n\n  const { state, dispatch } = ctx\n\n  const openPanel = useCallback((def: PanelDefinition) => {\n    const ps = buildInitialState(def, state.nextOrder)\n    dispatch({ type: 'OPEN_PANEL', state: ps })\n  }, [dispatch, state.nextOrder])\n\n  const closePanel = useCallback((id: string) => {\n    dispatch({ type: 'CLOSE_PANEL', id })\n  }, [dispatch])\n\n  const dockPanel = useCallback((id: string, zone: DockZone) => {\n    dispatch({ type: 'DOCK_PANEL', id, zone })\n  }, [dispatch])\n\n  const focusPanel = useCallback((id: string) => {\n    dispatch({ type: 'FOCUS_PANEL', id })\n  }, [dispatch])\n\n  const movePanel = useCallback((id: string, rect: PanelRect) => {\n    dispatch({ type: 'MOVE_PANEL', id, rect })\n  }, Record<string, unknown>) => {\n    dispatch({ type: 'SET_PANEL_PROPS', id, props })\n  }, [dispatch])\n\n  const getZonePanels = useCallback((zone: DockZone): PanelState[] => {\n    switch (zone) {\n      case 'center': return state.center\n      case 'right': return state.right\n      case 'bottom': return state.bottom\n      case 'floating': return state.floating\n    }\n  }, [state])\n\n  return }\n}","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

{"tool":"newFileCreated","path":"frontend/src/panel/contract.ts","content":"// ╔═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n// ║  UIContract — interface between panels and the shell             ║\n// ╚═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n\n/**\n * UIContract defines the strict, limited API that panels receive from AppShell.\n *\n * Panels NEVER access the store directly — they receive this contract as props.\n * This ensures panels are pure, testable, and interchangeable.\n */\n\nexport interface UIContract<PayloadType = Record<string, unknown>> {\n  // ── Data ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  /** The panel's payload (event data, service, geojson, etc.) */\n  payload: PayloadType\n\n  // ── Lifecycle ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n  /** Panel was opened */\n  onOpen?: () => void\n  /** Panel was closed */\n  onClose?: () => void\n  /** Panel received focus */\n  onFocus?: () => void\n  /** Panel was docked to a different zone */\n  onDock?: (zone: string) => void\n\n  // ── Communication ──────────────────────────────────────────\n  /** Emit an event to the shell's event bus (UI only) */\n  emit: (eventType: string, data: Record<string, unknown>) => void\n  /** Subscribe to events from other panels */\n  subscribe: (eventType: string, handler: (data: Record<string, unknown>) => void) => () => void\n\n  // ── Shell actions ──────────────────────────────────────────\n  /** Request to open a new panel by definition id */\n  openPanel: (panelId: string, props?: Record<string, unknown>) => void\n  /** Open the inspector with data */\n  inspect: (data: Record<string, unknown>) => void\n  /** Resize the panel */\n  resize: (w: number, h: number) => void\n}","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

'./types'\nimport { resolvePanelState } from './registry'\nimport { useDock } from './useDock'\n\ninterface PanelFrameProps {\n  state: PanelState\n  /** Optional event bus for UIContract  border: '1px solid #21262d', borderRadius: 6, overflow: 'hidden',\n        background: '#0d1117',\n      }}\n    >\n      {/* ── Title Bar ──────────────────────────────────────────── */}\n      <div\n        style={{\n          display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between',\n          padding: '0 8px', height: 30, flexShrink: 0, cursor: 'pointer',\n          background: state.active ? '#1c2128' : '#0d1117',\n          borderBottom: '1px solid #21262d',\n        }}\n      >\n        <span style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: '#e6edf3', userSelect: 'none' }}>\n          {resolved.Component.displayName || (resolved.Component as any).name || state.componentKey}\n        </span>\n\n        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 2 }}>\n          {/* Dock buttons */}\n          {state.zone !== 'center' && (\n            <ZoneBtn label=\"◫\" title=\"Dock center\" onClick={() => dockPanel(state.id, 'center')} />\n          )}\n          {state.zone !== 'right' && (\n            <ZoneBtn label=\"▦\" title=\"Dock right\" onClick={() => dockPanel(state.id, 'right')} />\n          )}\n          {state.zone !== 'bottom' && (\n            <ZoneBtn label=\"▬\" title=\"Dock bottom\" onClick={() => dockPanel(state.id, 'bottom')} />\n          )}\n\n          {isTabbed && (\n            <ZoneBtn label={isCollapsed ? '▸' : '▾'} title=\"Collapse\"\n                     onClick={() => collapsePanel(state.id)} />\n          )}\n\n          <ZoneBtn label=\"×\" title=\"Close\" onClick={() => closePanel(state.id)} />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* ── Panel Content ───────────────────────────────────────── */}\n      {!isCollapsed && (\n        <div style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'auto' }}>\n          {createElement(Component, panelProps)}\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction ZoneBtn({ label, title, onClick }: { label: string; title: string; onClick: () => void }) {\n  return (\n    <button\n      title={title}\n      onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onClick() }}\n      style={{\n        background: 'none', border: 'none', color: '#8b949e', cursor: 'pointer',\n        fontSize: 11, padding: '2px 4px', borderRadius: 2, lineHeight: 1,\n      }}\n      onMouseOver={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#e6edf3')}\n      onMouseOut={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#8b949e')}\n    >\n      {label}\n    </button>\n  )\n}","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%' }}>\n        {/* ── Children (main view) when panel is collapsed ────── */}\n        {active.collapsed && children && (\n          <div style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'auto' }}>{children}</div>\n        )}\n\n        {/* ── Tab Bar ─────────────────────────────────────────── */}\n        {panels.length > 1 && (\n          <div '#e6edf3' : '#8b949e',\n                  fontWeight: p.id === active.id ? 600 : 400,\n                }}\n              >\n                {p.componentKey}\n              </div>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {/* ── Active Panel Frame ──────────────────────────────── */}\n        {!active.collapsed && (\n          <div style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden' }}>\n            <PanelFrame state={active} />\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {/* ── Inactive (collapsed in tab bar) ─────────────────── */}\n        {inactive.map(p => (\n          <div key={p.id} style={{ display: 'none' }} />\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    )\n  }\n\n  // ── Right / Bottom / Floating: stacked panels ────────────────────\n  const vertical = zone === 'right'\n  const sizeStyle: Record<string, string | number> = vertical\n    ? { width: 340, flexShrink: 0 }\n    : { height: 200, flexShrink: 0 }\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{\n      display: 'flex',\n      flexDirection: vertical ? ### Tour 1274 — Assistant

*2026-06-17 07:52:08 UTC*

## ✅ OSIRIS v2 Panel System — Dockable Panel Architecture

**Build status :** `osiris-osiris-frontend Built` ✅

### 1. Panel System Structure

```
frontend/src/panel/
│
├── types.ts        ← DockZone, PanelKind, PanelDefinition, PanelState, DockLayout
├── registry.ts     ← registerPanel(), resolvePanelState(), buildInitialState()
├── useDock.ts      ← DockProvider + useDock() hook (reducer 7 actions)
├── contract.ts     ← UIContract interface (panels NEVER access store directly)
├── PanelFrame.tsx  ← Chrome wrapper (title bar, dock buttons, collapse/close)
└── PanelZone.tsx   ← Zone renderer (center=tabbed, right=stacked, bottom=stacked)
```

### 2. Panel Lifecycle

```
OPEN ──→ AppShell calls useDock().openPanel(def)
           │
           ▼
    DockProvider reducer: OPEN_PANEL
           │
           ▼
    buildInitialState(def, index) → PanelState { zone, rect, active, visible, ... }
           │
           ▼
    PanelZone renders PanelFrame(state)
           │
           ▼
    PanelFrame resolves Component via registry → UIContract(emit, subscribe, inspect, ...)
           │
    ┌──────┼──────┐
    ▼      ▼      ▼
  FOCUS  DOCK   CLOSE
  (tab)  (move  (dispatch
         zone)  CLOSE_PANEL)
```

### 3. Dock Zones

| Zone | Layout | Comportement |
|------|--------|-------------|
| `center` | Tabbed | Un seul panel actif à la fois, tab bar en haut |
| `right` | Stacked vertical | Panel frame 340px width |
| `bottom` | Stacked horizontal | Panel frame 200px height |
| `floating` | Free position | Panel frame draggable (future) |

### 4. Panel Types

```typescript
type PanelKind = 'stream' | 'map' | 'graph' | 'table' | 'inspector' | 'workflow'
```

### 5. UIContract — Communication

```typescript
interface UIContract {
  payload: PayloadType
  emit: (eventType, data) => void              // shell event bus
  subscribe: (eventType, handler) => () => void // unsubscribe
  openPanel: (panelId, props?) => void
  inspect: (data) => void
  resize: (w, h) => void
  onOpen?: () => void
  onClose?: () => void
  onFocus?: () => void
}
```

**Les panels ne lisent jamais le store directement** — ils reçoivent le `UIContract` en props. Cela garantit qu'ils sont purs, testables et interchangeables.

### 6. Panel Registry — Module Definitions

```typescript
import { registerPanel } from './panel/registry'
import EventMonitor from '../EventMonitor'

registerPanel(
  { id: 'soc-events', kind: 'stream', title: 'SOC Events', icon: '⚡',
    defaultZone: 'center', componentKey: 'soc-events' },
  EventMonitor
)

registerPanel(
  { id: 'geoint-map', kind: 'map', title: 'GEOINT Map', icon: '🌍',
    defaultZone: 'right', componentKey: 'geoint-map' },
  PlaceholderMap
)
```

### 7. Integration avec AppShell

```tsx
import { DockProvider } from './panel/useDock'
import PanelZone from './panel/PanelZone'

function AppShell() {
  return (
    <DockProvider>
      <TopBar />
      <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex' }}>
        <PanelZone zone="center">
          <HomeView />   {/* Fallback quand aucun panel center */}
        </PanelZone>
        <PanelZone zone="right" />
      </div>
      <PanelZone zone="bottom" />
      <EventStrip />
    </DockProvider>
  )
}
```

### 8. Dock Buttons (dans PanelFrame)

Chaque panel a une title bar avec :
| Bouton | Action |
|--------|--------|
| `◫` | Dock center (tabbed) |
| `▦` | Dock right (stacked) |
| `▬` | Dock bottom (stacked) |
| `▾/▸` | Collapse/Expand (center only) |
| `×` | Close panel |HAS_CHANGES

{"tool":"newFileCreated","path":"frontend/src/views/soc/AlertCluster.tsx","content":"import { useMemo } from 'react'\n\ninterface StreamEvent {\n  id: string; type: string; source: string; severity: string; timestamp: string; payload: Record<string, unknown>\n}\n\nexport default function AlertCluster({ events, onSelect }: { events: StreamEvent[]; onSelect: (e: StreamEvent) => void }) {\n  // Group by type + source\n  const clusters = useMemo(() => {\n    const map = new Map<string, { type: string; source: string; count: number; maxSeverity: string; events: StreamEvent[] }>()\n    for (const e of events) {\n      const key = `${e.type}:${e.source}`\n      const existing = map.get(key)\n      if (existing) {\n        existing.count++\n        existing.events.push(e)\n        if (e.severity === 'critical' || (e.severity === 'error' && existing.maxSeverity !== 'critical')) {\n          existing.maxSeverity = e.severity\n        }\n      } else {\n        map.set(key, { type: e.type, source: e.source, count: 1, maxSeverity: e.severity, events: [e] })\n      }\n    }\n    return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count).slice(0, 20)\n  }, [events])\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ padding: 8 }}>\n      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, color: '#58a6ff', marginBottom: 8 }}>Alert Clusters</div>\n\n      {clusters.map((c, i) => (\n        <div key={i} onClick={() => onSelect(c.events[0])}\n          style={{ padding: '4px 8px', borderBottom: '1px solid #21262d', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 11 }}>\n          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>\n            <span style={{\n              display: 'inline-block', width: 20, height: 20, borderRadius: 4,\n              background: '#1c2128', textAlign: 'center', lineHeight: '20px',\n              fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, color: c.count > 5 ? '#f85149' : '#d29922',\n            }}>{c.count}</span>\n            <span style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', color: '#e6edf3' }}>{c.type}</span>\n            <span style={{ color: '#8b949e' }}>{c.source?.replace('osiris-', '')}</span>\n            <span style={{ color: c.maxSeverity === 'critical' ? '#f85149' : '#d29922', fontWeight: 600 }}>{c.maxSeverity}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ))}\n\n      {clusters.length === 0 && (\n        <div style={{ padding: 12, color: '#484f58', fontSize: 12 }}>No alert clusters detected</div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  )\n}","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

## ✅ SOC Mode — Security Operations Center (intégré dans le système)

**Build status :** `osiris-osiris-frontend Built` ✅

### 1. SOC Layout (3 colonnes dans le Main Canvas)

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ TopBar │ Home │ SOC ● │ GEOINT │ Graph │ Workflow │ Events │ Score: 87/100  │
├──────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────┬───────────────────────┤
│                                  │                   │                       │
│  EVENT STREAM (flex:2)           │ INCIDENT TIMELINE │  AI SUGGESTIONS        │
│                                  │ (flex:1)          │  (flex:1)              │
│  TIME     │ TYPE     │ SOURCE    ├───────────────────┤                       │
│ ──────────│──────────│────────── │ P2 | Redis timeout│ Worker restart pattern │
│  12:34:01 │ fail     │ worker-ai │ P3 | AI crash loop│ detected: inc cooldown│
│  12:33:05 │ error    │ backend   │ P3 | Gateway spike│                   87% │
│  12:31:22 │ warn     │ graph     │ P4 | GEOINT review│                       │
│  12:30:00 │ ok       │ gateway   │                   │ Alert noise 92% —     │
│                                  │ ALERT CLUSTERS    │ filter info-level     │
│  [Filter events...]              │ (flex:1)          │                   68% │
│  142 / 1200 events               │                   │                       │
│                                  │ [5] container.die │ 3 incidents share     │
│                                  │ [3] health.fail   │ root cause "redis"    │
│                                  │ [2] timeout       │                   91% │
├──────────────────────────────────┴───────────────────┴───────────────────────┤
│ Live Events │ 12:34:01 │ container.died │ worker-ai │ ...                    │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 2. Fichiers créés

```
frontend/src/views/soc/
├── SocView.tsx          ← Layout 3 colonnes + tabs internes
├── EventStream.tsx      ← Live feed avec filtre texte
├── IncidentTimeline.tsx ← Timeline des 4 incidents seed
├── AlertCluster.tsx     ← Clusterisation par type+source
└── AiSuggestions.tsx    ← 4 suggestions avec confidence score
```

### 3. Data Flow

```
AppShell WebSocket
    │
    ▼
useAppStore.pushDockerEvent / pushBusEvent
    │
    ▼
SocView reçoit busEvents + dockerEvents
    │
    ├─ EventStream: filtre + trie + affiche 200 events max
    ├─ IncidentTimeline: statique (seed) → connecté à osiris:incidents
    ├─ AlertCluster: useMemo → groupBy(type+source) → top 20 clusters
    └─ AiSuggestions: statique (seed) → connecté à Decision/Incident engines
    │
    ▼
Click event → useAppStore.openInspector(data) → InspectorPanel (right drawer)
```

### 4. Interaction Model

| Action | Résultat |
|--------|----------|
| Click row in EventStream | Column data → `InspectorPanel` (right drawer, JSON view) |
| Click incident in Timeline | (Future) → Incident detail panel |
| Type in filter input | Live filter on event.type + event.source |
| TopBar → SOC | Render `SocView` in Main Canvas (view='soc') |
| EventStrip bottom | Réutilise le composant shell existant |

### 5. Intégration avec le Panel System

Le SOC Mode est une `View` du shell, pas un panel. Il peut être converti en panels dockables :
- `EventStream` → panel type `stream` dockable en `center`
- `AlertCluster` → panel type `table` dockable en `right`
- `AiSuggestions` → panel type `inspector` dockable en `right`

```typescript
registerPanel({ id: 'soc-stream', kind: 'stream', ... }, EventStream)
registerPanel({ id: 'soc-alerts', kind: 'table', ... }, AlertCluster)
registerPanel({ id: 'soc-suggestions', kind: 'inspector', ... }, AiSuggestions)
```HAS_CHANGES

           return <line key={`hg${i}`} x1={0} y1={y} x2={dims.w} y2={y} stroke=\"#161b22\" strokeWidth={1} />\n          })}\n\n          {/* Continent outlines (simplified) */}\n          {[\n            // Eurasia + Africa\n            { points: [[10, 55], [30, 60], [40, 55], [50, 50], [100, 50], [120, 40], [140, 35], [130, 20], [100, 0], [80, 10], [40, 5], [10, 0], [0, 10], [-10, 20], [-10, 40], [0, 50], [10, 55]] },\n            // America\n            { points: [[-130, 50], [-100, 50], [-80, 40], [-80, 20], [-90, 10], [-100, 0], [-80, -10], [-60, -20], [-50, -30], [-70, -40], [-80, -50], [-120, -50], [-130, -40], [-140, -30], [-130, 10], [-130, 30], [-130, 50]] },\n            // Australia\n            { points: [[110, -20], [130, -10], [150, -20], [155, -30], [150, -40], [130, -40], [110, -30], [110, -20]] },\n          ].map((cont, i) => (\n            <polygon\n              key={i} points={cont.points.map(([lon, lat]) => `${lonToX(lon, dims.w)},${latToY(lat, dims.h)}`).join(' ')}\n              fill=\"#1c2128\" stroke=\"#21262d\" strokeWidth={1}\n            />\n          ))}\n\n          {/* Event points */}\n          {points.map(p => {\n            const cx = lonToX(p.lon, dims.w)\n            const cy = latToY(p.lat, dims.h)\n            const isHovered = hovered === p.id\n            const r = isHovered ? 10 : 6\n\n            return (\n              <g key={p.id} onClick={() => openInspector(p)}\n                onMouseEnter={() => setHovered(p.id)} onMouseLeave={() => setHovered(null)}\n                style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}>\n                {/* Pulse ring */}\n                <circle cx={cx} cy={cy} r={r + 4} fill=\"none\" stroke={sevColor(p.severity)} strokeWidth={1} opacity={isHovered ? 0.8 : 0.3}>\n                  {isHovered && <animate attributeName=\"r\" values={`${r+4};${r+12};${r+4}`} dur=\"2s\" repeatCount=\"indefinite\" />}\n                </circle>\n                {/* Point */}\n                <circle cx={cx} cy={cy} r={r} fill={sevColor(p.severity)} opacity={0.9} />\n                {/* Label tooltip */}\n                {isHovered && (\n                  <g>\n                    <rect x={cx - 60} y={cy - r - 32} width={120} height={24} rx={4} fill=\"#1c2128\" stroke=\"#30363d\" />\n                    <text x={cx} y={cy - r - 16} textAnchor=\"middle\" fill=\"#e6edf3\" fontSize={10}>{p.label}</text>\n                  </g>\n                )}\n              </g>\n            )\n          })}\n        </svg>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Event List (bottom) */}\n      <div style={{ height: 100, <div key={p.id} onClick={() => openInspector(p)}\n            style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '60px 120px 1fr 80px', gap: 4, padding: '3px 8px', fontSize: 10, cursor: 'pointer', '#e6edf3', ui: '#1f6feb',\n}\n\nexport default function GraphView() {\n  const { openInspector } = useAppStore()\n  const [nodes] = useState(SEED_NODES)\n  const [edges] = useState(SEED_EDGES)\n simple force layout simulation\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const p: Record<string, NodePos> = {}\n    const cx = dims.w / 2, cy = dims.h / 2\n    const r = Math.min(dims.w, dims.h) * 0.35\n    nodes.forEach((n, i) => {\n      const angle = (i / nodes.length) * Math.PI * 2\n      p[n.id] = { x: cx + r * Math.cos(angle), y: cy + r * Math.sin(angle) }\n    })\n    // Settle: pull connected nodes closer\n    for (let iter = 0; iter < 20; iter++) {\n      for (const e of edges) {\n        const s = p[e.source], t = p[e.target]\n        if (!s || !t) continue\n        const dx = t.x - s.x, dy = t.y - s.y\n        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1\n        const pull = 2 / (iter + 1)\n        s.x += dx / dist * pull\n        s.y += dy / dist * pull\n        t.x -= dx / dist * pull\n        t.y -= dy / dist * pull\n      }\n    }\n    setPositions(p)\n  }, [dims.w, dims.h])\n\n  // ResizeObserver\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const el = svgRef.current?.parentElement\n    if (!el) return\n    const ro = new ResizeObserver(([e]) => setDims({ w: e.contentRect.width, h: e.contentRect.height }))\n    ro.observe(el)\n    return () => ro.disconnect()\n  }, [])\n\n  const handleMouseDown = useCallback((id: string, e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault()\n    setDrag({ id, ox: e.clientX, oy: e.clientY })\n  }, [])\n\n  const handleMouseMove = useCallback((e: MouseEvent) => {\n    if (!drag) return\n    const dx = e.clientX - drag.ox, dy = e.clientY - drag.oy\n    setPositions(prev => ({ ...prev, [drag.id]: { x: prev[drag.id].x + dx, y: prev[drag.id].y + dy } }))\n    setDrag({ ...drag, ox: e.clientX, oy: e.clientY })\n  }, [drag])\n\n  const handleMouseUp = useCallback(() => setDrag(null), [])\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!drag) return\n    window.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove)\n    window.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp)\n    return () => { window.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove); window.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp) }\n  }, [drag, handleMouseMove, handleMouseUp])\n\n  const scoreColor = (s: number) => s >= 80 ? '#3fb950' : s >= 50 ? '#d29922' : '#f85149'\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%', overflow: 'hidden' }}>\n      {/* Toolbar */}\n      <div style={{\n        display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12,\n        padding: '6px 12px', borderBottom: '1px solid #21262d', flexShrink: 0,\n        fontSize: 11, color: '#8b949e',\n      }}>\n        <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: '#58a6ff' }}>🔗 Graph Engine</span>\n        <span>{nodes.length} nodes · {edges.length} edges</span>\n        <span style={{ marginLeft: 'auto' }}>\n          {['infra', 'ai', 'graph', 'core'].map(g => (\n            <span key={g} style={{ marginLeft: 8, padding: '2px 6px', borderRadius: 3, background: '#1c2128', color: GROUP_COLORS[g], fontSize: 10 }}>{g}</span>\n          ))}\n        </span>\n        <button onClick={() => setZoom(z => Math.min(2, z + 0.2))} style={{ background: '#1c2128', border: '1px solid #30363d', color: '#8b949e', padding: '2px 6px', borderRadius: 3, cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 10 }}>+</button>\n        <span style={{ fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: 10 }}>{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>\n        <button onClick={() => setZoom(z => Math.max(0.3, z - 0.2))} style={{ background: '#1c2128', border: '1px solid #30363d', color: '#8b949e', padding: '2px 6px', borderRadius: 3, cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: 10 }}>−</button>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Graph Canvas (SVG) */}\n      <div style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', position: 'relative' }}>\n        <svg ref={svgRef} width={dims.w} height={dims.h} style={{ background: '#0d1117' }}>\n          <g transform={`scale(${zoom})`}>\n            {/* Edges */}\n            {edges.map((e, i) => {\n              const s = positions[e.source], t = positions[e.target]\n              if (!s || !t) return null\n              const isHighlighted = hovered === e.source || hovered === e.target\n              return (\n                <g key={i}>\n                  <line x1={s.x} y1={s.y} x2={t.x} y2={t.y}\n                    stroke={isHighlighted ? '#58a6ff' : '#21262d'}\n                    strokeWidth={isHighlighted ? 2 : Math.max(1, e.weight / 5)}\n                    opacity={isHighlighted ? 0.8 : 0.4} />\n                  {/* Edge label midpoint */}\n                  <text x={(s.x + t.x) / 2} y={(s.y + t.y) / 2 - 4}\n                    textAnchor=\"middle\" fill=\"#484f58\" fontSize={8}>{e.label}</text>\n                </g>\n              )\n            })}\n\n            {/* Nodes */}\n            {nodes.map(n => {\n              const p = positions[n.id]\n              if (!p) return null\n              const isHovered = hovered === n.id\n              const r = isHovered ? 22 : 18\n              const grp = nodes.filter(x => x.group === n.group).length > 1 ? n.group : ''\n\n              return (\n                <g key={n.id}\n                  transform={`translate(${p.x}, ${p.y})`}\n                  onMouseEnter={() => setHovered(n.id)}\n                  onMouseLeave={() => setHovered(null)}\n                  onClick={() => openInspector(n)}\n                  style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}>\n                  {/* Node circle */}\n                  <circle r={r} fill={GROUP_COLORS[n.group] || '#8b949e'} opacity={isHovered ? 0.9 : 0.7}\n                    stroke={isHovered ? '#e6edf3' : 'none'} strokeWidth={2} />\n                  {/* Health score ring */}\n                  <circle r={r + 3} fill=\"none\" stroke={scoreColor(n.score)} strokeWidth={2} opacity={0.6} />\n                  {/* Node label */}\n                  <text y={r + 14} textAnchor=\"middle\" fill=\"#e6edf3\" fontSize={9} fontWeight={600}\n                    style={{ pointerEvents: 'none' }}>{n.label.replace('osiris-', '')}</text>\n                  <text y={r + 25} textAnchor=\"middle\" fill={scoreColor(n.score)} fontSize={8}\n                    style={{ pointerEvents: 'none' }}>score {n.score}</text>\n                </g>\n              )\n            })}\n          </g>\n        </svg>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Legend */}\n      <div style={{\n        height: 24, borderTop: '1px solid #21262d', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center',\n        padding: '0 12px', gap: 12, fontSize: 10, color: '#484f58', flexShrink: 0,\n      }}>\n        <span>Legend:</span>\n        {Object.entries(GROUP_COLORS).map(([g, c]) => (\n          <span key={g} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>\n            <span style={{ width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: 4, background: c, display: 'inline-block' }} />\n            {g}\n          </span>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

## ✅ GEOINT + Graph Engine UI — Intégrés dans le panel system

**Build status :** `osiris-osiris-frontend Built` ✅

### 1. Fichiers créés

```
frontend/src/views/
├── geoint/
│   └── GeointView.tsx       ← 280 lignes — interactive SVG map
└── graph/
    └── GraphView.tsx        ← 310 lignes — entity relationship graph
```

### 2. GEOINT — Architecture

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 🌍 GEOINT │ 5 points │ anomaly  signal  asset  satellite    │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                               │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │  SVG Mercator Projection (ResizeObserver responsive)     │  │
│  │                                                          │  │
│  │  · Grid lines (lat/lon)                                 │  │
│  │  · Continent outlines (polygons Eurasia+Africa/America/│  │
│  │    Australia)                                           │  │
│  │  · Event points (circles color=severity)               │  │
│  │  · Hover: pulse animation + tooltip label              │  │
│  │  · Click: openInspector(data)                          │  │
│  │                                                          │  │
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
│                                                               │
│ TIME  │ TYPE               │ LABEL                    │ SEV  │
│ 12:34 │ anomaly.movement   │ Paris — vehicle cluster  │ warn │
│ 12:28 │ signal.intercept   │ Tokyo — signal spike     │ info │
│ 12:15 │ asset.tracking     │ Sydney — vessel detected │ info │
│ 11:50 │ anomaly.movement   │ London — pattern dev     │ error│
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Features :**
- Carte SVG Mercator pure (0 dépendances carto)
- 5 seed points (Paris, Tokyo, Sydney, London, Mumbai)
- Pulse animation au hover
- Surface-tooltip avec label
- ResizeObserver → adaptatif
- Event list en bas → click → InspectorPanel

### 3. Graph Engine — Architecture

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 🔗 Graph Engine │ 12 nodes · 13 edges │ infra ai graph core │+│−│
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                               │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │
│  │  SVG Graph (force layout + drag + zoom)                 │  │
│  │                                                          │  │
│  │  ┌─────┐    ┌───────┐    ┌──────┐                       │  │
│  │  │redis│ ←──│backend│←───│gatewy│                       │  │
│  │  └─────┘    └───────┘    └──────┘                       │  │
│  │    ↑            ↑                                       │  │
│  │  ┌────────┐  ┌──────────┐                               │  │
│  │  │ai-workr│  │graph-work│                               │  │
│  │  └──┬─────┘  └────┬─────┘                               │  │
│  │  ┌──▼──┐  ┌─────▼──┐                                    │  │
│  │  │qdrant│  │memgraph│                                    │  │
│  │  └──────┘  └────────┘                                    │  │
│  │                                                          │  │
│  │  · Nodes: circle + color(group) + score ring            │  │
│  │  · Edges: weight-based stroke + midpoint label           │  │
│  │  · Hover: highlight connected edges                     │  │
│  │  · Drag: free reposition nodes                          │  │
│  │  · Zoom: +/- buttons (30% → 200%)                       │  │
│  │  · Click: openInspector(data)                           │  │
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │
│                                                               │
│ Legend: ● infra  ● networking  ● core  ● ai  ● graph  ● ... │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Features :**
- 12 nœuds représentant les services Osiris réels
- 13 arêtes avec labels ("depends on", "routes to", "writes to", "stores to")
- Force layout simulation (20 itérations, attirance entre connectés)
- Drag & drop des nœuds
- Zoom (30%–200%)
- Highlight au hover (edges connectés s'illuminent)
- Score ring autour de chaque nœud
- Legende des groupes

|------|-----------|--------|
| `home` | `HomeView.tsx` | ✅ Dashboard minimal |
| `soc` | `SocView.tsx` + 4 sous-composants | ✅ 3 colonnes SOC |
| `geoint` | `GeointView.tsx` | ✅ Carte SVG + overlays |
| `graph` | `GraphView.tsx` | ✅ Force layout graph |
| `workflow` | `PlaceholderView` | 🚧 Placeholder |
| `events` | `EventsView.tsx` → `EventMonitor` | ✅ Composant existant |

### 5. Performance considerations

| Aspect | Stratégie |
|--------|-----------|
| **Large datasets (GEOINT)** | SVG batch rendering (10K points = 10K `<circle>`) — utilisez canvas si >5K |
| **Large datasets (Graph)** | Virtualisation des nœuds, canvas WebGL si >500 nœuds |
| **Force layout** | Simulation synchrone dans useEffect — passez à Worker ou d3-force si >50 nœuds |
| **Resize** | ResizeObserver — pas de polling DOM |
| **Memo** | `useMemo` pour le clustering dans AlertCluster — pas de recalcul si props inchangées |HAS_CHANGES

│ │ HEALTHY│healthy│ avail  │ ● conn │         │

│ └────────────┘ └────────────┘               │
